<!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
  <head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8" />
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0" />

    <!--
      ==================================================================
      DocuForge - index.html (single entry point for every route)
      ------------------------------------------------------------------
      This one file is served for EVERY URL (/, /merge-pdf, /blog/...)
      because the app is a client-side single-page app. After React
      loads, src/utils/seo.ts (applySEO) rewrites the title, description,
      canonical, Open Graph / Twitter tags, robots and JSON-LD for the
      current route. The tags below are therefore only the DEFAULTS that
      crawlers and link-previews see when they do not run JavaScript.

      Deliberately NOT set here: <link rel="canonical"> and og:url.
      Since this file is shared by all routes, a hard-coded canonical
      would wrongly point every page at the homepage. They are added
      per-route by JavaScript instead.

      BEFORE DEPLOYING: replace https://docuforge.app in the two image
      URLs below (og:image, twitter:image) with your real domain. Do the
      same in public/robots.txt. (VITE_SITE_URL in .env.example drives
      the sitemap and the per-route tags.)
      ==================================================================
    -->

    <title>DocuForge - PDF &amp; Document Tools</title>
    <meta name="description" content="Fast, secure, and private online tools to merge, split, compress, convert, rotate, OCR, and edit PDFs, images, and documents directly in your browser." />
    <meta name="robots" content="index, follow, max-image-preview:large, max-snippet:-1, max-video-preview:-1" />

    <!-- Appearance: the whole UI is dark (bg-slate-900 = #0f172a) -->
    <meta name="color-scheme" content="dark" />
    <meta name="theme-color" content="#0f172a" />
    <style>
      /* Prevents a white flash before the stylesheet and React load. */
      html { background-color: #0f172a; color-scheme: dark; }
    </style>

    <!-- Icons and web app manifest -->
    <link rel="icon" type="image/svg+xml" href="/favicon.svg" />
    <link rel="apple-touch-icon" sizes="180x180" href="/apple-touch-icon.png" />
    <link rel="manifest" href="/site.webmanifest" />
    <meta name="application-name" content="DocuForge" />
    <meta name="apple-mobile-web-app-title" content="DocuForge" />
    <meta name="format-detection" content="telephone=no" />

    <!-- Open Graph (link previews on Facebook, LinkedIn, WhatsApp, Slack, ...) -->
    <meta property="og:site_name" content="DocuForge" />
    <meta property="og:type" content="website" />
    <meta property="og:locale" content="en_US" />
    <meta property="og:title" content="DocuForge - PDF &amp; Document Tools" />
    <meta property="og:description" content="Fast, secure, and private online tools to merge, split, compress, convert, rotate, OCR, and edit PDFs, images, and documents directly in your browser." />
    <meta property="og:image" content="https://docuforge.app/og-image.png" />
    <meta property="og:image:type" content="image/png" />
    <meta property="og:image:width" content="1200" />
    <meta property="og:image:height" content="630" />
    <meta property="og:image:alt" content="DocuForge - free PDF and document tools that run privately in your browser" />

    <!-- Twitter / X -->
    <meta name="twitter:card" content="summary_large_image" />
    <meta name="twitter:title" content="DocuForge - PDF &amp; Document Tools" />
    <meta name="twitter:description" content="Fast, secure, and private online tools to merge, split, compress, convert, rotate, OCR, and edit PDFs, images, and documents directly in your browser." />
    <meta name="twitter:image" content="https://docuforge.app/og-image.png" />
    <meta name="twitter:image:alt" content="DocuForge - free PDF and document tools that run privately in your browser" />

    <!-- Fonts: connect early, load the stylesheet without blocking first paint -->
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com" />
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin />
    <link
      rel="stylesheet"
      href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Plus+Jakarta+Sans:wght@400;500;600;700;800&amp;family=JetBrains+Mono:wght@400;500&amp;display=swap"
      media="print"
      onload="this.media='all'"
    />
    <noscript>
      <link
        rel="stylesheet"
        href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Plus+Jakarta+Sans:wght@400;500;600;700;800&amp;family=JetBrains+Mono:wght@400;500&amp;display=swap"
      />
    </noscript>

    <!-- Default structured data. Replaced per route by applySEO() (same id). -->
    <script type="application/ld+json" id="page-jsonld">
      {
        "@context": "https://schema.org",
        "@type": "SoftwareApplication",
        "name": "DocuForge",
        "operatingSystem": "Any (runs in a web browser)",
        "applicationCategory": "BusinessApplication",
        "offers": {
          "@type": "Offer",
          "price": "0",
          "priceCurrency": "USD"
        },
        "description": "All-in-one web document utility suite for PDF merging, splitting, compressing, page manipulation, image conversions, and OCR."
      }
    </script>
  </head>

  <body class="bg-slate-900 text-slate-100 antialiased selection:bg-indigo-500 selection:text-white">
    <!-- Shown only when JavaScript is disabled; gives crawlers and users real content and links. -->
    <noscript>
      <div style="max-width:720px;margin:0 auto;padding:48px 20px;font-family:system-ui,-apple-system,'Segoe UI',Roboto,sans-serif;color:#e2e8f0;line-height:1.6">
        <h1 style="font-size:28px;margin:0 0 12px;color:#ffffff">DocuForge - PDF &amp; Document Tools</h1>

        <nav aria-label="Popular tools">

            <li><a href="/merge-pdf" style="color:#a5b4fc">Merge PDF</a></li>

            <li><a href="/jpg-to-pdf" style="color:#a5b4fc">JPG to PDF</a></li>
            <li><a href="/image-to-text" style="color:#a5b4fc">Image to Text (OCR)</a></li>
            <li><a href="/tools" style="color:#a5b4fc">Browse all tools</a></li>
          </ul>
          <p style="margin:0;font-size:14px">
            <a href="/blog" style="color:#a5b4fc">Blog</a> &middot;
            <a href="/privacy" style="color:#a5b4fc">Privacy</a> &middot;
            <a href="/terms" style="color:#a5b4fc">Terms</a> &middot;
            <a href="/contact" style="color:#a5b4fc">Contact</a>
          </p>

======================================================================
FILE: .env.example
======================================================================
# DocuForge is a 100% client-side, static web app: there is no backend server,
# no database, and no API keys of any kind. Every PDF/image/OCR tool runs
# entirely in the visitor's browser. Because of that, there is exactly one
# environment variable this project actually reads:

# The production domain this site is deployed at. Used to build canonical URLs,
# Open Graph/Twitter tags, and JSON-LD structured data (see src/config/seo.ts).
# Set this in your hosting provider's environment variables before deploying.
VITE_SITE_URL="https://docuforge.app"

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Everything below is NOT an environment variable in this codebase -- these are
# plain constants in source, listed here only so you know where to change them:
#
#   Support/contact email  -> src/pages/ContactPage.tsx   (SUPPORT_EMAIL)
#   Admin demo passcode    -> src/pages/AdminDashboardPage.tsx
#   Per-tool max file size -> src/config/tools.ts          (maxFileSizeMB per tool)
#   Sitemap domain         -> public/robots.txt and scripts/generate-sitemap.ts
#                              (the script also reads VITE_SITE_URL above)
#
# There is no server-side file retention or analytics backend to configure:
# analyticsService.ts logs job events only to the browser's own localStorage.

======================================================================
FILE: README.md
======================================================================
# DocuForge — PDF & Document Tools

42 browser-based tools (PDF, converters, images, OCR, utilities). All file processing runs
locally in the browser — your file content is never uploaded to a server.

This is a **Vite + React + TypeScript** single-page app styled with **Tailwind CSS v4**.
It is not a Next.js project and has no backend, database or API keys.

## Run locally
Requires Node.js **22.13 or newer** (pdfjs-dist 6 needs it).
```bash
npm install
npm run dev        # http://localhost:3000
npm run build      # regenerates public/sitemap.xml, then builds to dist/
npm run preview    # serve the production build locally
```
Open the site through the dev server (or a deployed URL). Double-clicking `index.html`
does not work: the browser cannot run `/src/main.tsx` (TypeScript/JSX) without Vite.

## How everything is connected
```
index.html                      the only HTML file; every URL is served by it
 └─ <div id="root">             the mount point
 └─ <script type="module" src="/src/main.tsx">
      src/main.tsx
       ├─ import './index.css'  -> @import "tailwindcss" + @theme (fonts, brand colors)
       └─ <App />               src/App.tsx: reads the URL (history API), picks a page,
            │                   and calls applySEO() to set title/meta/canonical/JSON-LD
            ├─ pages (lazy)     HomePage, ToolDirectoryPage, ToolPage, BlogPage,
            │                   LegalPage, ContactPage, AdminDashboardPage
            ├─ components       Navbar, Footer, SearchModal, FileUploader, ResultCard, ...
            ├─ config           tools.ts (42 tools, titles, FAQs), blog.ts, seo.ts
            └─ services         pdfEngine, renderEngine, conversionEngine, imageEngine,
                                ocrEngine (pdf-lib, pdf.js, jszip, docx, tesseract.js)
```
| File | Role |
|---|---|
| `package.json` | dependencies and scripts (`dev`, `build`, `seo:sitemap`, `lint`) |
| `vite.config.ts` | enables the React and Tailwind plugins (`@vitejs/plugin-react`, `@tailwindcss/vite`) |
| `src/index.css` | Tailwind v4 is configured here (`@theme`). There is **no** `tailwind.config.*` file — v4 does not need one |
| `src/App.tsx` | the router: `/` home, `/tools`, `/blog`, `/blog/<slug>`, `/privacy`, `/terms`, `/security`, `/contact`, `/admin`, `/<tool-slug>` |
| `src/pages/BlogPage.tsx` | renders both the blog list and a single post (there is no separate `BlogPostPage`) |
| `vercel.json`, `public/_redirects` | rewrite every path to `index.html` so direct links work; `vercel.json` also sets security headers |
| `public/` | `favicon.svg`, icons, `og-image.png`, `site.webmanifest`, `robots.txt`, `sitemap.xml` |
| `scripts/generate-sitemap.ts` | builds `public/sitemap.xml` from `tools.ts` and `blog.ts` (runs before every build) |

To add a tool: add an entry to `src/config/tools.ts`, handle its `id` in the `switch` in
`src/pages/ToolPage.tsx`, then run `npm run seo:sitemap`.

## Deploy
Static site (Vercel framework preset "Vite": build command `npm run build`, output `dist`).
Set the environment variable `VITE_SITE_URL` to your real domain (see `.env.example`).

## Before launch — things you must set
- Replace the placeholder domain `https://docuforge.app` in `public/robots.txt` and in the
  two image URLs (`og:image`, `twitter:image`) of `index.html`.
- `src/pages/ContactPage.tsx` → replace `SUPPORT_EMAIL` with your real inbox.
  The form opens the visitor's email app (`mailto:`); there is no backend to receive it.
- `src/pages/AdminDashboardPage.tsx` → change `ADMIN_PASSCODE`. This is a light
  client-side gate only (visible in the JS bundle), not real authentication.
  The dashboard shows per-browser history, not site-wide analytics.
- Run `npm install` once so the lockfiles include the `tsx` dev dependency.
  (Keep only one lockfile in use: `package-lock.json` for npm or `bun.lock` for bun.)

## Known limitations (stated honestly in the UI too)
- OCR and some PDF rendering fetch engine data (fonts, language model) from a public CDN
  (jsDelivr) at runtime. File contents are never sent.
- Title, canonical, Open Graph and JSON-LD tags are set by JavaScript per page. Search
  engines that run JavaScript (Google) see them; link previews from crawlers that do not
  run JavaScript fall back to the defaults in `index.html`. Prerendering would fix that.
- PDF → Word/Excel/PowerPoint are best-effort text/position extractions:
  Excel output is CSV; PowerPoint output is a ZIP of slide images + outline (not .pptx).
- Word → PDF extracts text/paragraphs from .docx (no images/tables/fonts).
  Excel → PDF reads the cell values of every worksheet in an .xlsx (no formulas/charts),
  showing at most 8 columns per sheet.
- PDF text output supports Latin characters; other scripts are replaced with "?" in
  generated PDFs (watermark, Word/Excel/slides → PDF). Word and OCR text outputs keep all scripts.
- Unlock PDF: files that only restrict permissions are rebuilt with text intact; files that
  need a password to open require that password and are rebuilt from page images (text is
  no longer selectable). Other PDF tools stop with a message when a file is encrypted.
- Sign PDF places a drawn image signature; it is not a cryptographic digital signature.
- Compress PDF: "High/Recommended" may rasterize pages when structural optimization
  can't shrink the file (text becomes non-selectable; the result panel says so).

FILE: src/App.tsx
======================================================================
import { useState, useEffect, Suspense, lazy } from 'react';
import { Navbar } from './components/Navbar';
import { Footer } from './components/Footer';
import { SearchModal } from './components/SearchModal';
import { TOOLS_CONFIG } from './config/tools';
import { BLOG_POSTS } from './config/blog';
import { ToolCategory } from './types';
import { applySEO } from './utils/seo';
import {
  SITE_NAME,
  absoluteUrl,
  buildOrganizationJsonLd,
  buildWebsiteJsonLd,
  buildToolJsonLd,
  buildBlogPostingJsonLd,
  buildBreadcrumbJsonLd
} from './config/seo';

// Lazy-loaded so each page's own dependencies (pdf-lib, tesseract.js, docx, etc.)
// only download when that page is actually visited, instead of all landing in
// the initial bundle every visitor has to fetch to see the homepage.
const HomePage = lazy(() => import('./pages/HomePage').then((m) => ({ default: m.HomePage })));
const ToolDirectoryPage = lazy(() => import('./pages/ToolDirectoryPage').then((m) => ({ default: m.ToolDirectoryPage })));
const ToolPage = lazy(() => import('./pages/ToolPage').then((m) => ({ default: m.ToolPage })));
const BlogPage = lazy(() => import('./pages/BlogPage').then((m) => ({ default: m.BlogPage })));
const LegalPage = lazy(() => import('./pages/LegalPage').then((m) => ({ default: m.LegalPage })));
const ContactPage = lazy(() => import('./pages/ContactPage').then((m) => ({ default: m.ContactPage })));
const AdminDashboardPage = lazy(() => import('./pages/AdminDashboardPage').then((m) => ({ default: m.AdminDashboardPage })));

export default function App() {
  const [currentPath, setCurrentPath] = useState<string>(() => {
    return window.location.pathname || '/';
  });
  const [searchOpen, setSearchOpen] = useState(false);

  // Sync route on popstate
  useEffect(() => {
    const handlePopState = () => {
      setCurrentPath(window.location.pathname || '/');
    };
    window.addEventListener('popstate', handlePopState);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('popstate', handlePopState);
  }, []);

  const navigate = (path: string) => {
    if (path === currentPath) return;
    window.history.pushState({}, '', path);
    setCurrentPath(path);
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
  };

  // SEO: title, meta description, canonical, Open Graph/Twitter tags, robots and
  // JSON-LD structured data are all kept in sync with the current route here.
  // See src/utils/seo.ts for the documented limitation of doing this client-side
  // in a non-prerendered SPA.
  useEffect(() => {
    const cleanPath = currentPath.split('?')[0].replace(/^\//, '');

    // 1. Individual tool page (e.g. /merge-pdf, /compress-pdf)
    const matchedTool = TOOLS_CONFIG.find((t) => t.slug === cleanPath);
    if (matchedTool) {
      applySEO({
        title: matchedTool.seoTitle,
        description: matchedTool.seoDescription,
        canonical: absoluteUrl(`/${matchedTool.slug}`),
        jsonLd: buildToolJsonLd(matchedTool)
      });
      return;
    }

    // 2. Tool directory — every ?cat= filter is a view of the same page, so they all
    // canonicalize to the bare /tools URL to avoid duplicate-content variants.
    if (cleanPath === 'tools') {
      applySEO({
        title: 'All PDF & Document Tools Catalog | DocuForge',
        description:
          'Browse every free DocuForge tool: merge, split, compress and edit PDFs, convert between PDF, Word, Excel, PowerPoint and images, and run OCR — all processed privately in your browser.',
        canonical: absoluteUrl('/tools'),
        jsonLd: buildBreadcrumbJsonLd([
          { name: 'Home', path: '/' },
          { name: 'All Tools', path: '/tools' }
        ])
      });
      return;
    }

    // 3. Blog index or an individual post
    if (cleanPath === 'blog' || cleanPath.startsWith('blog/')) {
      const slug = cleanPath.split('/')[1];
      const post = slug ? BLOG_POSTS.find((p) => p.slug === slug) : undefined;

      if (slug && !post) {
        applySEO({
          title: 'Article Not Found | DocuForge',
          description: 'This article could not be found. Browse the DocuForge knowledge base for other guides.',
          canonical: absoluteUrl('/blog'),
          robots: 'noindex, follow',
          jsonLd: null
        });
        return;
      }

      if (post) {
        applySEO({
          title: `${post.title} | DocuForge Blog`,
          description: post.description,
          canonical: absoluteUrl(`/blog/${post.slug}`),
          ogType: 'article',
          jsonLd: buildBlogPostingJsonLd(post)
        });
        return;
      }

      applySEO({
        title: 'DocuForge Knowledge Base - Guides, Tutorials & Best Practices',
        description:
          'Practical, in-depth guides on compressing, merging, converting and managing PDF and document files — written by the DocuForge team.',
        canonical: absoluteUrl('/blog'),
        jsonLd: buildBreadcrumbJsonLd([
          { name: 'Home', path: '/' },
          { name: 'Blog', path: '/blog' }
        ])
      });
      return;
    }

    // 4. Legal & trust pages
    if (cleanPath === 'privacy') {
      applySEO({
        title: 'Privacy Policy & Zero Retention Guarantee | DocuForge',
        description:
          'How DocuForge handles your files: in-browser processing, no document uploads, and no server-side retention of your file content.',
        canonical: absoluteUrl('/privacy'),
        jsonLd: buildBreadcrumbJsonLd([
          { name: 'Home', path: '/' },
          { name: 'Privacy Policy', path: '/privacy' }
        ])
      });
      return;
    }
    if (cleanPath === 'security') {
      applySEO({
        title: 'Security Architecture | DocuForge',
        description:
          'A plain-language look at how DocuForge validates and processes files client-side, and what that does and doesn\u2019t protect against.',
        canonical: absoluteUrl('/security'),
        jsonLd: buildBreadcrumbJsonLd([
          { name: 'Home', path: '/' },
          { name: 'Security', path: '/security' }
        ])
      });
      return;
    }
    if (cleanPath === 'terms') {
      applySEO({
        title: 'Terms of Service | DocuForge',
        description: 'The terms that govern your use of DocuForge\u2019s free online PDF and document tools.',
        canonical: absoluteUrl('/terms'),
        jsonLd: buildBreadcrumbJsonLd([
          { name: 'Home', path: '/' },
          { name: 'Terms of Service', path: '/terms' }
        ])
      });
      return;
    }

    // 5. Contact
    if (cleanPath === 'contact') {
      applySEO({
        title: 'Contact Support & Feedback | DocuForge',
        description:
          'Questions about a document format, a bug report, or a tool request \u2014 get in touch with the DocuForge team.',
        canonical: absoluteUrl('/contact'),
        jsonLd: buildBreadcrumbJsonLd([
          { name: 'Home', path: '/' },
          { name: 'Contact', path: '/contact' }
        ])
      });
      return;
    }

    // 6. Admin dashboard — a demo/internal page, deliberately kept out of search results
    if (cleanPath === 'admin') {
      applySEO({
        title: 'Admin Dashboard | DocuForge',
        description: 'Internal dashboard showing the tool history stored in this browser. Not intended for search engines.',
        canonical: absoluteUrl('/admin'),
        robots: 'noindex, nofollow',
        jsonLd: null
      });
      return;
    }

    // 7. Homepage
    if (cleanPath === '') {
      applySEO({
        title: `${SITE_NAME} - PDF & Document Tools`,
        description:
          'Fast, secure, and private online tools to merge, split, compress, convert, rotate, OCR, and edit PDFs, images, and documents directly in your browser.',
        canonical: absoluteUrl('/'),
        jsonLd: [buildOrganizationJsonLd(), buildWebsiteJsonLd()]
      });
      return;
    }

    // 8. Anything else is a genuine unknown route — keep it out of the index.
    applySEO({
      title: 'Page Not Found | DocuForge',
      description: 'The page you requested could not be found.',
      canonical: absoluteUrl(currentPath.split('?')[0]),
      robots: 'noindex, follow',
      jsonLd: null
    });
  }, [currentPath]);

  // Route Resolver
  const renderCurrentRoute = () => {
    const [pathPart, queryPart] = currentPath.split('?');
    const cleanSlug = pathPart.replace(/^\//, '');

    // 1. Home
    if (cleanSlug === '' || cleanSlug === 'index.html') {
      return <HomePage onNavigate={navigate} />;
    }

    // 2. Tool Directory
    if (cleanSlug === 'tools') {
      const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(queryPart || '');
      const catParam = (urlParams.get('cat') as ToolCategory) || 'all';
      return <ToolDirectoryPage initialCategory={catParam} onNavigate={navigate} />;
    }

    // 3. Individual Tool Page (e.g. /merge-pdf, /compress-pdf)
    const tool = TOOLS_CONFIG.find((t) => t.slug === cleanSlug);
    if (tool) {
      return <ToolPage tool={tool} onNavigate={navigate} />;
    }

    // 4. Blog & Articles
    if (cleanSlug.startsWith('blog')) {
      const parts = cleanSlug.split('/');
      const blogSlug = parts[1];
      return <BlogPage currentSlug={blogSlug} onNavigate={navigate} />;
    }

    // 5. Legal Pages
    if (['privacy', 'terms', 'security'].includes(cleanSlug)) {
      return <LegalPage type={cleanSlug as any} onNavigate={navigate} />;
    }

    // 6. Contact
    if (cleanSlug === 'contact') {
      return <ContactPage />;
    }

    // 7. Admin Dashboard
    if (cleanSlug === 'admin') {
      return <AdminDashboardPage />;
    }

    // Fallback: 404 Not Found
    const popularTools = TOOLS_CONFIG.filter((t) => t.popular).slice(0, 6);
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-2xl px-4 py-24 text-center space-y-6">
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <h1 className="text-4xl font-extrabold text-white">404</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-400">The tool or page you requested could not be found.</p>
          <button
            onClick={() => navigate('/')}
            className="rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 px-5 py-2.5 text-xs font-semibold text-white hover:bg-indigo-500"
          >
            Return Home
          </button>
        </div>
        {popularTools.length > 0 && (
          <div className="pt-6 border-t border-slate-800">
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 mb-3">Or try a popular tool</p>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-2">
              {popularTools.map((t) => (
                <button
                  key={t.slug}
                  onClick={() => navigate(`/${t.slug}`)}
                  className="rounded-lg bg-slate-800 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-700 hover:text-white transition-colors"
                >
                  {t.name}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    );
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col bg-slate-900 text-slate-100 font-sans selection:bg-indigo-500 selection:text-white">
      {/* Navbar */}
      <Navbar
        currentPath={currentPath}
        onNavigate={navigate}
        onOpenSearch={() => setSearchOpen(true)}
      />

      {/* Main Content Viewport */}
      <main className="flex-1">
        <Suspense
          fallback={
            <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-32">
              <div className="h-8 w-8 animate-spin rounded-full border-2 border-indigo-500 border-t-transparent" />
            </div>
          }
        >
          {renderCurrentRoute()}
        </Suspense>
      </main>

      {/* Footer */}
      <Footer onNavigate={navigate} />

      {/* Quick Search Modal (⌘K) */}
      <SearchModal
        isOpen={searchOpen}
        onClose={() => setSearchOpen(false)}
        onSelectTool={(slug) => {
          if (slug === 'OPEN_SEARCH_EVENT') {
            setSearchOpen(true);
          } else {
            navigate(`/${slug}`);
          }
        }}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/FAQAccordion.tsx
======================================================================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { ChevronDown } from 'lucide-react';
import { ToolFAQ } from '../types';

interface FAQAccordionProps {
  faqs: ToolFAQ[];
}

export const FAQAccordion: React.FC<FAQAccordionProps> = ({ faqs }) => {
  const [openIndices, setOpenIndices] = useState<number[]>([0]);

  if (!faqs || faqs.length === 0) return null;

  const toggle = (index: number) => {
    setOpenIndices((prev) =>
      prev.includes(index) ? prev.filter((i) => i !== index) : [...prev, index]
    );
  };

  return (
    <div className="w-full space-y-3">
      <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-white tracking-tight">Frequently Asked Questions</h3>
      <div className="divide-y divide-slate-800 rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/60 overflow-hidden">
        {faqs.map((faq, idx) => {
          const isOpen = openIndices.includes(idx);
          return (
            <div key={idx} className="transition-colors">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => toggle(idx)}
                className="flex w-full items-center justify-between p-4 text-left text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold text-slate-200 hover:text-white"
              >
                <span>{faq.question}</span>
                <ChevronDown
                  className={`h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-slate-400 transition-transform duration-200 ${
                    isOpen ? 'rotate-180 text-indigo-400' : ''
                  }`}
                />
              </button>
              {isOpen && (
                <div className="px-4 pb-4 pt-1 text-xs sm:text-sm leading-relaxed text-slate-400">
                  {faq.answer}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

======================================================================
FILE: src/components/FileUploader.tsx
======================================================================
import React, { useRef, useState, DragEvent, ChangeEvent } from 'react';
import { UploadCloud, File as FileIcon, X, ArrowUp, ArrowDown } from 'lucide-react';
import { ToolDefinition, UploadedFileItem } from '../types';
import { formatBytes } from '../utils/formatters';
import { validateUploadedFiles, validateFileSignatures } from '../utils/fileValidation';

interface FileUploaderProps {
  tool: ToolDefinition;
  files: UploadedFileItem[];
  onFilesChanged: (files: UploadedFileItem[]) => void;
  onError: (msg: string | null) => void;
  disabled?: boolean;
}

export const FileUploader: React.FC<FileUploaderProps> = ({
  tool,
  files,
  onFilesChanged,
  onError,
  disabled = false
}) => {
  const [isDragging, setIsDragging] = useState(false);
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  const handleDragOver = (e: DragEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (disabled) return;
    setIsDragging(true);
  };

  const handleDragLeave = (e: DragEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setIsDragging(false);
  };

  const handleDrop = (e: DragEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setIsDragging(false);
    if (disabled) return;

    if (e.dataTransfer.files && e.dataTransfer.files.length > 0) {
      processIncomingFiles(Array.from(e.dataTransfer.files));
    }
  };

  const handleFileInputChange = (e: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    if (e.target.files && e.target.files.length > 0) {
      processIncomingFiles(Array.from(e.target.files));
      // Reset input value so re-selecting same file triggers change
      e.target.value = '';
    }
  };

  const processIncomingFiles = async (newRawFiles: File[]) => {
    onError(null);

    // Merge or replace depending on maxFiles
    let candidateList: File[];
    if (tool.maxFiles === 1) {
      candidateList = [newRawFiles[0]];
    } else {
      const existingRaw = files.map((f) => f.file);
      candidateList = [...existingRaw, ...newRawFiles].slice(0, tool.maxFiles);
    }

    const validation = validateUploadedFiles(candidateList, tool);
    if (!validation.valid) {
      onError(validation.errorMessage || 'Invalid file uploaded.');
      return;
    }

    // Only check the bytes of files that are newly added (already-accepted files were
    // checked when they were first dropped in, and re-reading them here is wasted work).
    const existingFileObjects = new Set(files.map((f) => f.file));
    const newlyAdded = candidateList.filter((f) => !existingFileObjects.has(f));
    const signatureCheck = await validateFileSignatures(newlyAdded);
    if (!signatureCheck.valid) {
      onError(signatureCheck.errorMessage || 'This file could not be verified as a valid file of its type.');
      return;
    }

    // Transform into UploadedFileItems
    const newItems: UploadedFileItem[] = candidateList.map((file) => {
      const existing = files.find((f) => f.file === file);
      if (existing) return existing;

      let previewUrl: string | undefined = undefined;
      if (file.type.startsWith('image/')) {
        previewUrl = URL.createObjectURL(file);
      }

      return {
        id: 'file-' + Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 9),
        file,
        name: file.name,
        size: file.size,
        type: file.type || 'application/octet-stream',
        previewUrl
      };
    });

    onFilesChanged(newItems);
  };

  const removeFile = (id: string) => {
    const item = files.find((f) => f.id === id);
    if (item?.previewUrl) {
      URL.revokeObjectURL(item.previewUrl);
    }
    const filtered = files.filter((f) => f.id !== id);
    onFilesChanged(filtered);
    onError(null);
  };

  const moveFile = (index: number, direction: 'up' | 'down') => {
    const targetIndex = direction === 'up' ? index - 1 : index + 1;
    if (targetIndex < 0 || targetIndex >= files.length) return;

    const copy = [...files];
    const [moved] = copy.splice(index, 1);
    copy.splice(targetIndex, 0, moved);
    onFilesChanged(copy);
  };

  const acceptString = tool.acceptedFileTypes.includes('*') ? undefined : tool.acceptedFileTypes.join(',');

  return (
    <div className="w-full space-y-4">
      {/* Upload Dropzone */}
      {files.length === 0 || tool.maxFiles > 1 ? (
        <div
          onDragOver={handleDragOver}
          onDragLeave={handleDragLeave}
          onDrop={handleDrop}
          onClick={() => !disabled && fileInputRef.current?.click()}
          className={`group relative flex cursor-pointer flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border-2 border-dashed p-8 text-center transition-all ${
            isDragging
              ? 'border-indigo-500 bg-indigo-500/10'
              : 'border-slate-700 bg-slate-800/40 hover:border-indigo-500/50 hover:bg-slate-800/80'
          } ${disabled ? 'cursor-not-allowed opacity-50' : ''}`}
        >
          <input
            ref={fileInputRef}
            type="file"
            multiple={tool.maxFiles > 1}
            accept={acceptString}
            onChange={handleFileInputChange}
            disabled={disabled}
            className="hidden"
          />

          <div className="flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-indigo-600/20 text-indigo-400 group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform">
            <UploadCloud className="h-7 w-7" />
          </div>

          <h3 className="mt-4 text-base font-semibold text-white">
            {files.length > 0 ? 'Add more files' : 'Drop your file here'}
          </h3>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-400">
            or <span className="font-semibold text-indigo-400 underline decoration-indigo-400/40">browse from your computer</span> or phone
          </p>

          <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-2 text-[11px] text-slate-400">
            <span className="rounded bg-slate-800 px-2 py-0.5 border border-slate-700/60">
              Accepted: {tool.acceptedFileTypes.join(', ')}
            </span>
            <span className="rounded bg-slate-800 px-2 py-0.5 border border-slate-700/60">
              Max size: {tool.maxFileSizeMB} MB
            </span>
            {tool.maxFiles > 1 && (
              <span className="rounded bg-slate-800 px-2 py-0.5 border border-slate-700/60">
                Up to {tool.maxFiles} files
              </span>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : null}

      {/* Uploaded File List */}
      {files.length > 0 && (
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-medium text-slate-400 px-1">
            <span>
              {files.length} {files.length === 1 ? 'file' : 'files'} selected
            </span>
            {tool.maxFiles > 1 && (
              <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                Use arrows to reorder pages
              </span>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-2">
            {files.map((item, index) => (
              <div
                key={item.id}
                className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-800/80 p-3 transition-colors hover:border-slate-700"
              >
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
                  {item.previewUrl ? (
                    <img
                      src={item.previewUrl}
                      alt={item.name}
                      className="h-10 w-10 shrink-0 rounded-lg object-cover border border-slate-700"
                    />
                  ) : (
                    <div className="flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-indigo-500/10 text-indigo-400 border border-indigo-500/20">
                      <FileIcon className="h-5 w-5" />
                    </div>
                  )}

                  <div className="min-w-0">
                    <p className="truncate text-xs font-semibold text-slate-200">
                      {item.name}
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                      {formatBytes(item.size)}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center gap-1 shrink-0 ml-3">
                  {tool.maxFiles > 1 && (
                    <>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => moveFile(index, 'up')}
                        disabled={index === 0 || disabled}
                        className="rounded p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:bg-slate-700 hover:text-white disabled:opacity-30"
                        title="Move Up"
                      >
                        <ArrowUp className="h-4 w-4" />
                      </button>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => moveFile(index, 'down')}
                        disabled={index === files.length - 1 || disabled}
                        className="rounded p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:bg-slate-700 hover:text-white disabled:opacity-30"
                        title="Move Down"
                      >
                        <ArrowDown className="h-4 w-4" />
                      </button>
                    </>
                  )}

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => removeFile(item.id)}
                    disabled={disabled}
                    className="rounded p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:bg-rose-500/20 hover:text-rose-400 disabled:opacity-30 transition-colors"
                    title="Remove file"
                  >
                    <X className="h-4 w-4" />
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

                <button onClick={() => onNavigate('/terms')} className="hover:text-indigo-400">Terms of Service</button>
              </li>
              <li>
                <button onClick={() => onNavigate('/security')} className="hover:text-indigo-400">Security Architecture</button>

            </ul>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Bottom copyright */}
        <div className="mt-12 flex flex-col items-center justify-between border-t border-slate-800/80 pt-6 sm:flex-row">
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">
            &copy; {new Date().getFullYear()} DocuForge. Built for private, high-fidelity document workflows.
          </p>
          <div className="mt-4 flex items-center gap-4 text-xs text-slate-400 sm:mt-0">
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
              Encrypted Client Session
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </footer>
  );
};

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  FileText,
  Layers,
  RefreshCw,
  Image as ImageIcon,
  ScanText,
  Sliders,
  Menu,
  X,
  Search
} from 'lucide-react';

interface NavbarProps {
  currentPath: string;
  onNavigate: (path: string) => void;
  onOpenSearch: () => void;
}

export const Navbar: React.FC<NavbarProps> = ({ currentPath, onNavigate, onOpenSearch }) => {
  const [mobileMenuOpen, setMobileMenuOpen] = useState(false);

  const navLinks = [
    { label: 'PDF Tools', path: '/tools?cat=pdf', icon: Layers },
    { label: 'Converter', path: '/tools?cat=converter', icon: RefreshCw },
    { label: 'Image Tools', path: '/tools?cat=image', icon: ImageIcon },
    { label: 'OCR', path: '/tools?cat=ocr', icon: ScanText },
    { label: 'Utilities', path: '/tools?cat=utilities', icon: Sliders },
    { label: 'Blog', path: '/blog', icon: FileText }
  ];

  const handleNav = (path: string) => {
    onNavigate(path);
    setMobileMenuOpen(false);
  };

  return (
    <header className="sticky top-0 z-40 w-full border-b border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/90 backdrop-blur-md">
      <div className="mx-auto flex h-16 max-w-7xl items-center justify-between px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        {/* Brand Logo */}
        <div
          onClick={() => handleNav('/')}
          onKeyDown={(e) => (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') && handleNav('/')}
          role="button"
          tabIndex={0}
          aria-label="DocuForge home"
          className="flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-2.5 transition-transform hover:scale-[1.01]"
        >
          <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-tr from-indigo-600 via-indigo-500 to-blue-500 shadow-md shadow-indigo-500/20">
            <FileText className="h-5 w-5 text-white" />
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-col">
            <span className="text-lg font-extrabold tracking-tight text-white">
              Docu<span className="text-indigo-400">Forge</span>
            </span>
            <span className="text-[10px] font-medium uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400">
              PDF & Document Suite
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Desktop Navigation */}
        <nav className="hidden items-center gap-1 md:flex">
          {navLinks.map((link) => {
            const Icon = link.icon;
            const isActive = currentPath === link.path || (link.path.startsWith('/tools') && currentPath.includes(link.path.split('?')[1] || ''));
            return (
              <button
                key={link.label}
                onClick={() => handleNav(link.path)}
                className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium transition-colors ${
                  isActive
                    ? 'bg-slate-800 text-indigo-400'
                    : 'text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-800/60 hover:text-white'
                }`}
              >
                <Icon className="h-4 w-4 opacity-75" />
                {link.label}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </nav>

        {/* Action Controls */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 sm:gap-3">
          <button
            onClick={onOpenSearch}
            className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-slate-800 bg-slate-800/70 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-400 transition-colors hover:border-slate-700 hover:text-white"
            title="Search tools"
          >
            <Search className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-slate-400" />
            <span className="hidden sm:inline">Search tools...</span>
            <kbd className="hidden rounded bg-slate-900 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] text-slate-400 lg:inline">⌘K</kbd>
          </button>

          <button
            onClick={() => handleNav('/tools')}
            className="hidden items-center rounded-lg bg-indigo-600 px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white shadow-sm transition hover:bg-indigo-500 sm:flex"
          >
            All Tools
          </button>

          {/* Mobile menu trigger */}
          <button
            onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(!mobileMenuOpen)}
            className="rounded-lg p-2 text-slate-400 hover:bg-slate-800 hover:text-white md:hidden"
            aria-label="Toggle menu"
          >
            {mobileMenuOpen ? <X className="h-6 w-6" /> : <Menu className="h-6 w-6" />}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Mobile Drawer */}
      {mobileMenuOpen && (
        <div className="border-b border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 px-4 pt-2 pb-6 md:hidden">
          <div className="space-y-1">
            {navLinks.map((link) => {
              const Icon = link.icon;
              return (
                <button
                  key={link.label}
                  onClick={() => handleNav(link.path)}
                  className="flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-left text-sm font-medium text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-800 hover:text-white"
                >
                  <Icon className="h-4 w-4 text-indigo-400" />
                  {link.label}
                </button>
              );
            })}
            <div className="pt-2">
              <button
                onClick={() => handleNav('/tools')}
                className="w-full rounded-lg bg-indigo-600 py-2.5 text-center text-sm font-semibold text-white shadow-sm hover:bg-indigo-500"
              >
                Browse All Tools
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </header>
  );
};

FILE: src/components/PageThumbnailGrid.tsx

import React from 'react';
import { RotateCw, Trash2, ArrowLeft, ArrowRight, CheckSquare, Square } from 'lucide-react';
import { PDFPageInfo } from '../types';

interface PageThumbnailGridProps {
  pages: PDFPageInfo[];
  mode: 'rotate' | 'delete' | 'extract' | 'reorder';
  onRotatePage?: (pageNumber: number) => void;
  onToggleSelect?: (pageNumber: number) => void;
  onMovePage?: (index: number, direction: 'left' | 'right') => void;
  onSelectAll?: () => void;
  onDeselectAll?: () => void;
  onRotateAll?: () => void;
}

export const PageThumbnailGrid: React.FC<PageThumbnailGridProps> = ({
  pages,
  mode,
  onRotatePage,
  onToggleSelect,
  onMovePage,
  onSelectAll,
  onDeselectAll,
  onRotateAll
}) => {
  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      {/* Action Toolbar */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b border-slate-800 pb-3">
        <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300">
          Total Pages: <span className="text-indigo-400">{pages.length}</span>
          {(mode === 'delete' || mode === 'extract') && (
            <span className="ml-2 text-slate-400">
              ({pages.filter((p) => p.selected).length} selected)
            </span>
          )}
        </span>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          {mode === 'rotate' && onRotateAll && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onRotateAll}
              className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-700 bg-slate-800 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-700 hover:text-white"
            >
              <RotateCw className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              Rotate All 90°
            </button>
          )}

          {(mode === 'delete' || mode === 'extract') && (
            <>
              {onSelectAll && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={onSelectAll}
                  className="rounded-lg border border-slate-700 bg-slate-800 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-700"
                >
                  Select All
                </button>
              )}
              {onDeselectAll && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={onDeselectAll}
                  className="rounded-lg border border-slate-700 bg-slate-800 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-700"
                >
                  Clear Selection
                </button>
              )}
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Grid of Pages */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3 md:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-5">
        {pages.map((p, index) => {
          const isSelected = p.selected;

          return (
            <div
              key={p.pageNumber}
              onClick={() => (mode === 'delete' || mode === 'extract') && onToggleSelect?.(p.pageNumber)}
              onKeyDown={(e) => {
                if ((mode === 'delete' || mode === 'extract') && (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ')) {
                  e.preventDefault();
                  onToggleSelect?.(p.pageNumber);
                }
              }}
              role={mode === 'delete' || mode === 'extract' ? 'button' : undefined}
              tabIndex={mode === 'delete' || mode === 'extract' ? 0 : undefined}
              aria-pressed={mode === 'delete' || mode === 'extract' ? isSelected : undefined}
              className={`group relative flex flex-col items-center rounded-xl border p-2.5 transition-all ${
                mode === 'delete' && isSelected
                  ? 'border-rose-500/70 bg-rose-500/10'
                  : mode === 'extract' && isSelected
                  ? 'border-indigo-500 bg-indigo-500/10 shadow-md shadow-indigo-500/10'
                  : 'border-slate-800 bg-slate-800/50 hover:border-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-800'
              } ${(mode === 'delete' || mode === 'extract') ? 'cursor-pointer' : ''}`}
            >
              {/* Header Badge */}
              <div className="mb-2 flex w-full items-center justify-between text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                <span className="font-semibold text-slate-300">#{p.pageNumber}</span>

                {(mode === 'delete' || mode === 'extract') && (
                  <span className={isSelected ? (mode === 'delete' ? 'text-rose-400' : 'text-indigo-400') : 'text-slate-400'}>
                    {isSelected ? <CheckSquare className="h-4 w-4" /> : <Square className="h-4 w-4" />}
                  </span>
                )}

                {mode === 'rotate' && p.rotation > 0 && (
                  <span className="rounded bg-indigo-500/20 px-1 py-0.2 text-[10px] font-bold text-indigo-400">
                    {p.rotation}°
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* Thumbnail Container */}
              <div className="relative flex h-36 w-full items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-lg bg-white p-1 shadow-inner">
                {p.thumbnailUrl ? (
                  <img
                    src={p.thumbnailUrl}
                    alt={`Page ${p.pageNumber}`}
                    className="max-h-full max-w-full object-contain transition-transform duration-200"
                    style={{ transform: `rotate(${p.rotation}deg)` }}
                  />
                ) : (
                  <div
                    className="flex h-full w-full flex-col justify-between rounded border border-slate-200 bg-white p-2 text-slate-800 transition-transform duration-200"
                    style={{ transform: `rotate(${p.rotation}deg)` }}
                  >
                    <div className="h-2 w-3/4 rounded bg-slate-300" />
                    <div className="space-y-1">
                      <div className="h-1.5 w-full rounded bg-slate-200" />
                      <div className="h-1.5 w-5/6 rounded bg-slate-200" />
                      <div className="h-1.5 w-2/3 rounded bg-slate-200" />
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-center font-bold text-xs text-slate-400">
                      Page {p.pageNumber}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* Delete overlay indicator */}
                {mode === 'delete' && isSelected && (
                  <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center bg-rose-950/60 backdrop-blur-[1px]">
                    <Trash2 className="h-8 w-8 text-rose-400" />
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* Bottom Actions */}
              <div className="mt-2.5 flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-1.5">
                {mode === 'rotate' && onRotatePage && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={(e) => {
                      e.stopPropagation();
                      onRotatePage(p.pageNumber);
                    }}
                    className="flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-1 rounded bg-slate-700/80 py-1 text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-200 hover:bg-indigo-600 hover:text-white transition-colors"
                  >
                    <RotateCw className="h-3 w-3" />
                    Rotate 90°
                  </button>
                )}

                {mode === 'reorder' && onMovePage && (
                  <div className="flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-1">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={(e) => {
                        e.stopPropagation();
                        onMovePage(index, 'left');
                      }}
                      disabled={index === 0}
                      className="flex-1 rounded bg-slate-700/80 py-1 text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-600 disabled:opacity-20 flex justify-center"
                      title="Move Left"
                    >
                      <ArrowLeft className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={(e) => {
                        e.stopPropagation();
                        onMovePage(index, 'right');
                      }}
                      disabled={index === pages.length - 1}
                      className="flex-1 rounded bg-slate-700/80 py-1 text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-600 disabled:opacity-20 flex justify-center"
                      title="Move Right"
                    >
                      <ArrowRight className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

FILE: src/components/ProgressBar.tsx
======================================================================
import React from 'react';
import { Loader2, CheckCircle2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { ProcessingState } from '../types';

interface ProgressBarProps {
  state: ProcessingState;
  progressPercent?: number; // 0 to 100
  customMessage?: string;
}

export const ProgressBar: React.FC<ProgressBarProps> = ({
  state,
  progressPercent = 50,
  customMessage
}) => {
  if (state === 'idle') return null;

  const steps: Array<{ key: ProcessingState; label: string }> = [
    { key: 'uploading', label: 'Uploading' },
    { key: 'analyzing', label: 'Analyzing' },
    { key: 'processing', label: 'Processing' },
    { key: 'finalizing', label: 'Finalizing' },
    { key: 'completed', label: 'Complete' }
  ];

  const getStepIndex = (s: ProcessingState) => {
    switch (s) {
      case 'uploading':
        return 0;
      case 'analyzing':
        return 1;
      case 'processing':
        return 2;
      case 'finalizing':
        return 3;
      case 'completed':
        return 4;
      default:
        return 2;
    }
  };

  const currentIndex = getStepIndex(state);

  return (
    <div className="w-full rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/90 p-6 shadow-xl">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
          {state === 'completed' ? (
            <CheckCircle2 className="h-5 w-5 text-emerald-400" />
          ) : (
            <Loader2 className="h-5 w-5 text-indigo-400 animate-spin" />
          )}
          <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-white">
            {customMessage || (state === 'completed' ? 'Processing Complete' : `${steps[currentIndex]?.label}...`)}
          </span>
        </div>
        <span className="text-xs font-mono font-medium text-indigo-400">
          {Math.round(progressPercent)}%
        </span>
      </div>

      {/* Progress Bar Track */}
      <div className="h-2 w-full overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-slate-800">
        <div
          className="h-full rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-500 via-blue-500 to-emerald-400 transition-all duration-300 ease-out"
          style={{ width: `${Math.min(100, Math.max(5, progressPercent))}%` }}
        />
      </div>

      {/* Step Indicators */}
      <div className="mt-5 grid grid-cols-5 text-center text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-400">
        {steps.map((step, idx) => {
          const isPassed = idx < currentIndex;
          const isCurrent = idx === currentIndex;
          return (
            <div key={step.key} className="flex flex-col items-center">
              <span
                className={`mb-1 flex h-4 w-4 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-[9px] font-bold ${
                  isPassed
                    ? 'bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-400'
                    : isCurrent
                    ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white'
                    : 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-400'
                }`}
              >
                {isPassed ? '✓' : idx + 1}
              </span>
              <span
                className={`hidden sm:inline ${
                  isCurrent ? 'text-indigo-400 font-semibold' : isPassed ? 'text-slate-300' : 'text-slate-400'
                }`}
              >
                {step.label}
              </span>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

FILE: src/components/RelatedTools.tsx
======================================================================
import React from 'react';
import { ArrowRight } from 'lucide-react';
import { TOOLS_CONFIG } from '../config/tools';
import { getToolIcon } from '../utils/toolIcon';

interface RelatedToolsProps {
  currentSlug: string;
  relatedSlugs: string[];
  onNavigate: (slug: string) => void;
}

export const RelatedTools: React.FC<RelatedToolsProps> = ({ currentSlug, relatedSlugs, onNavigate }) => {
  // Find related tools from configuration
  let tools = TOOLS_CONFIG.filter((t) => relatedSlugs.includes(t.slug) && t.slug !== currentSlug);

  // If none configured, fallback to 4 other popular tools
  if (tools.length === 0) {
    tools = TOOLS_CONFIG.filter((t) => t.slug !== currentSlug).slice(0, 4);
  }

  return (
    <div className="w-full space-y-4">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-white tracking-tight">Related Document Tools</h3>
        <button
          onClick={() => onNavigate('/tools')}
          className="text-xs font-semibold text-indigo-400 hover:text-indigo-300 flex items-center gap-1"
        >
          View all tools <ArrowRight className="h-3 w-3" />
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
        {tools.slice(0, 4).map((tool) => {
          const Icon = getToolIcon(tool.iconName);
          return (
            <div
              key={tool.id}
              onClick={() => onNavigate(`/${tool.slug}`)}
              onKeyDown={(e) => (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') && onNavigate(`/${tool.slug}`)}
              role="button"
              tabIndex={0}
              className="group cursor-pointer rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/60 p-4 transition-all hover:border-indigo-500/50 hover:bg-slate-800/80 hover:shadow-lg hover:shadow-indigo-500/5"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <div className="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-indigo-500/10 text-indigo-400 group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform">
                  <Icon className="h-4 w-4" />
                </div>
                <div className="min-w-0">
                  <h4 className="truncate text-xs font-bold text-slate-200 group-hover:text-indigo-400 transition-colors">
                    {tool.name}
                  </h4>
                  <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">
                    {tool.category}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>
              <p className="mt-2 line-clamp-2 text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                {tool.shortDescription}
              </p>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

FILE: src/components/ResultCard.tsx
======================================================================
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Download, CheckCircle2, Copy, Check, RotateCcw, Sparkles } from 'lucide-react';
import confetti from 'canvas-confetti';
import { ProcessedResult } from '../types';
import { formatBytes } from '../utils/formatters';

interface ResultCardProps {
  result: ProcessedResult;
  onReset: () => void;
}

export const ResultCard: React.FC<ResultCardProps> = ({
  result,
  onReset
}) => {
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    // Launch celebratory confetti burst
    try {
      confetti({
        particleCount: 60,
        spread: 70,
        origin: { y: 0.6 }
      });
    } catch {
      // Ignore if canvas-confetti is unsupported
    }
  }, []);

  const handleCopyText = async () => {
    if (!result.extractedText) return;
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(result.extractedText);
      setCopied(true);
      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
    } catch {
      // fallback
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="w-full rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/90 p-6 sm:p-8 shadow-2xl space-y-6">
      {/* Header status */}
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center text-center">
        <div className="flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400 border border-emerald-500/20 mb-3 shadow-inner">
          <CheckCircle2 className="h-7 w-7" />
        </div>
        <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-white tracking-tight">Your file is ready!</h3>
        <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-400">
          Processed securely in your browser session.
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Metrics Panel */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-800/40 p-4">
        <div>
          <span className="text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-400">Original Size</span>
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-200 mt-0.5">
            {formatBytes(result.originalSize)}
          </p>
        </div>

        <div>
          <span className="text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-400">Result Size</span>
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-emerald-400 mt-0.5">
            {formatBytes(result.newSize)}
          </p>
        </div>

        {result.reductionPercentage !== undefined && result.reductionPercentage > 0 ? (
          <div className="col-span-2 sm:col-span-1">
            <span className="text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-400">Reduction</span>
            <p className="flex items-center gap-1 text-sm font-bold text-emerald-400 mt-0.5">
              <Sparkles className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              {result.reductionPercentage}% saved
            </p>
          </div>
        ) : result.pageCount ? (
          <div className="col-span-2 sm:col-span-1">
            <span className="text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-400">Pages</span>
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-indigo-400 mt-0.5">
              {result.pageCount} pages
            </p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="col-span-2 sm:col-span-1">
            <span className="text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-400">Status</span>
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-indigo-400 mt-0.5">
              Ready
            </p>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Metadata / inspection details, if applicable */}
      {result.metadata && Object.keys(result.metadata).length > 0 && (
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <span className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-400 px-1">Details</span>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-x-4 gap-y-1.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 p-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
            {Object.entries(result.metadata).map(([key, value]) => (
              <div key={key} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs">
                <span className="text-slate-500">{key}</span>
                <span className="font-semibold text-slate-200">{value}</span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Text preview if applicable */}
      {result.extractedText && (
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-medium text-slate-400 px-1">
            <span>Extracted Content Preview</span>
            <button
              onClick={handleCopyText}
              className="flex items-center gap-1 text-indigo-400 hover:text-indigo-300 font-semibold"
            >
              {copied ? <Check className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-emerald-400" /> : <Copy className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />}
              {copied ? 'Copied to Clipboard' : 'Copy Text'}
            </button>
          </div>
          <div className="max-h-48 overflow-y-auto rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 p-4 font-mono text-xs text-slate-300 whitespace-pre-wrap leading-relaxed select-all">
            {result.extractedText}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Main Download CTA */}
      <div className="space-y-3">
        <a
          href={result.downloadUrl}
          download={result.fileName}
          className="flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 px-6 py-3.5 text-sm font-bold text-white shadow-lg shadow-indigo-600/30 transition-all hover:bg-indigo-500 hover:scale-[1.01] active:scale-[0.99]"
        >
          <Download className="h-5 w-5" />
          Download {result.fileName}
        </a>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onReset}
          className="flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-800/80 px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-800 hover:text-white transition-colors"
        >
          <RotateCcw className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
          Process Another File
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

======================================================================
FILE: src/components/SearchModal.tsx
======================================================================
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { Search, X, ArrowRight } from 'lucide-react';
import { TOOLS_CONFIG } from '../config/tools';
import { getToolIcon } from '../utils/toolIcon';

interface SearchModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSelectTool: (slug: string) => void;
}

export const SearchModal: React.FC<SearchModalProps> = ({ isOpen, onClose, onSelectTool }) => {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) {
      setTimeout(() => inputRef.current?.focus(), 50);
    } else {
      setQuery('');
    }
  }, [isOpen]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if ((e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey) && e.key === 'k') {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (isOpen) onClose();
        else onSelectTool('OPEN_SEARCH_EVENT');
      }
      if (e.key === 'Escape' && isOpen) {
        onClose();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
  }, [isOpen, onClose, onSelectTool]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const filteredTools = TOOLS_CONFIG.filter((t) => {
    const q = query.toLowerCase().trim();
    return (
      t.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      t.shortDescription.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      t.category.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      t.acceptedFileTypes.some((ext) => ext.toLowerCase().includes(q))
    );
  });

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-start justify-center bg-slate-950/80 p-4 pt-20 backdrop-blur-sm"
      onClick={onClose}
    >
      <div
        className="relative w-full max-w-2xl rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 shadow-2xl overflow-hidden animate-in fade-in zoom-in-95 duration-150"
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      >
        {/* Search Input Bar */}
        <div className="flex items-center border-b border-slate-800 px-4 py-3.5">
          <Search className="h-5 w-5 text-slate-400 shrink-0" />
          <input
            ref={inputRef}
            type="text"
            value={query}
            onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Search tools (e.g. compress, merge, word, ocr)..."
            className="ml-3 flex-1 bg-transparent text-sm text-white placeholder-slate-500 outline-none"
          />
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="rounded-lg p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:bg-slate-800 hover:text-white"
          >
            <X className="h-4 w-4" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Results List */}
        <div className="max-h-96 overflow-y-auto p-2 space-y-1">
          {filteredTools.length > 0 ? (
            filteredTools.map((tool) => {
              const Icon = getToolIcon(tool.iconName);
              return (
                <div
                  key={tool.id}
                  onClick={() => {
                    onSelectTool(tool.slug);
                    onClose();
                  }}
                  onKeyDown={(e) => {
                    if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {
                      e.preventDefault();
                      onSelectTool(tool.slug);
                      onClose();
                    }
                  }}
                  role="button"
                  tabIndex={0}
                  className="flex cursor-pointer items-center justify-between rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2.5 hover:bg-slate-800/80 transition-colors group"
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
                    <div className="flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-indigo-500/10 text-indigo-400 group-hover:bg-indigo-600 group-hover:text-white transition-colors">
                      <Icon className="h-4 w-4" />
                    </div>
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <p className="truncate text-xs font-semibold text-white group-hover:text-indigo-400 transition-colors">
                        {tool.name}
                      </p>
                      <p className="truncate text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                        {tool.shortDescription}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <span className="ml-2 flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-400 group-hover:text-indigo-400 shrink-0">
                    Open <ArrowRight className="h-3 w-3" />
                  </span>
                </div>
              );
            })
          ) : (
            <div className="p-8 text-center text-xs text-slate-400">
              No matching tools found for "{query}".
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Footer shortcuts */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-t border-slate-800 bg-slate-950/60 px-4 py-2 text-[11px] text-slate-400">
          <span>{filteredTools.length} tools available</span>
          <span>Press ESC to close</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

FILE: src/config/seo.ts
======================================================================
import { ToolDefinition } from '../types';

/**
 * The production domain. Set VITE_SITE_URL in your hosting provider's environment
 * variables (see .env.example) before deploying — this fallback is a placeholder only.
 */
export const SITE_URL: string = (
  (typeof import.meta !== 'undefined' && (import.meta as any).env?.VITE_SITE_URL) ||
  'https://docuforge.app'
).replace(/\/+$/, '');

export const SITE_NAME = 'DocuForge';

export function absoluteUrl(path: string): string {
  const clean = path.startsWith('/') ? path : `/${path}`;
  return `${SITE_URL}${clean}`;
}

export function buildOrganizationJsonLd() {
  return {
    '@context': 'https://schema.org',
    '@type': 'Organization',
    name: SITE_NAME,
    url: SITE_URL,
    sameAs: []
  };
}

export function buildWebsiteJsonLd() {
  return {
    '@context': 'https://schema.org',
    '@type': 'WebSite',
    name: SITE_NAME,
    url: SITE_URL
  };
}

export interface BreadcrumbItem {
  name: string;
  path: string; // relative path, e.g. '/', '/tools', '/merge-pdf'
}

export function buildBreadcrumbJsonLd(items: BreadcrumbItem[]) {
  return {
    '@context': 'https://schema.org',
    '@type': 'BreadcrumbList',
    itemListElement: items.map((item, index) => ({
      '@type': 'ListItem',
      position: index + 1,
      name: item.name,
      item: absoluteUrl(item.path)
    }))
  };
}

const CATEGORY_LABEL: Record<string, string> = {
  pdf: 'PDF Tools',
  converter: 'Converters',
  image: 'Image Tools',
  ocr: 'OCR Tools',
  utilities: 'Utilities'
};

export function categoryLabel(category: string): string {
  return CATEGORY_LABEL[category] || 'Tools';
}

/**
 * SoftwareApplication + (optional) FAQPage structured data for a single tool page.
 * Built entirely from the tool's own config (name, description, real FAQ content) —
 * nothing here is invented (no fake ratings, review counts, or prices beyond the
 * genuinely-free $0 offer every tool on this site actually has).
 */
export function buildToolJsonLd(tool: ToolDefinition) {
  const softwareApp = {
    '@context': 'https://schema.org',
    '@type': 'SoftwareApplication',
    name: `${tool.name} — ${SITE_NAME}`,
    url: absoluteUrl(`/${tool.slug}`),
    applicationCategory: 'BusinessApplication',
    operatingSystem: 'Any (runs in a web browser)',
    description: tool.shortDescription,
    offers: {
      '@type': 'Offer',
      price: '0',
      priceCurrency: 'USD'
    }
  };

  const breadcrumb = buildBreadcrumbJsonLd([
    { name: 'Home', path: '/' },
    { name: categoryLabel(tool.category), path: `/tools?cat=${tool.category}` },
    { name: tool.name, path: `/${tool.slug}` }
  ]);

  const nodes: object[] = [softwareApp, breadcrumb];

  if (tool.faqs && tool.faqs.length > 0) {
    nodes.push({
      '@context': 'https://schema.org',
      '@type': 'FAQPage',
      mainEntity: tool.faqs.map((faq) => ({
        '@type': 'Question',
        name: faq.question,
        acceptedAnswer: {
          '@type': 'Answer',
          text: faq.answer
        }
      }))
    });
  }

  return nodes;
}

export interface BlogPostLike {
  slug: string;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  date: string;
  author: string;
}

export function buildBlogPostingJsonLd(post: BlogPostLike) {
  return [
    {
      '@context': 'https://schema.org',
      '@type': 'BlogPosting',
      headline: post.title,
      description: post.description,
      datePublished: post.date,
      author: { '@type': 'Organization', name: post.author || SITE_NAME },
      publisher: { '@type': 'Organization', name: SITE_NAME },
      url: absoluteUrl(`/blog/${post.slug}`),
      mainEntityOfPage: absoluteUrl(`/blog/${post.slug}`)
    },
    buildBreadcrumbJsonLd([
      { name: 'Home', path: '/' },
      { name: 'Blog', path: '/blog' },
      { name: post.title, path: `/blog/${post.slug}` }
    ])
  ];
}
