FILE: src/pages/AdminDashboardPage.tsx
======================================================================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  BarChart3,
  CheckCircle2,
  HardDrive,
  Activity,
  Layers,
  RefreshCw,
  Lock
} from 'lucide-react';
import { AnalyticsService, JobRecord } from '../services/analyticsService';
import { formatBytes } from '../utils/formatters';

// NOTE: This is a lightweight client-side gate, not real authentication —
// with no backend, there is no way to truly secure this route (anyone can
// read this constant in the shipped JS bundle). It only stops the dashboard
// from being one accidental click away for an ordinary visitor. Real access
// control needs a server-side login (see Phase 2 of the architecture doc).
const ADMIN_PASSCODE = 'docuforge-admin';
const ADMIN_SESSION_KEY = 'docuforge_admin_unlocked';

export const AdminDashboardPage: React.FC = () => {
  const [unlocked, setUnlocked] = useState<boolean>(
    () => sessionStorage.getItem(ADMIN_SESSION_KEY) === 'true'
  );
  const [passcodeInput, setPasscodeInput] = useState('');
  const [passcodeError, setPasscodeError] = useState(false);

  const [stats, setStats] = useState(AnalyticsService.getAggregatedStats());
  const [jobs, setJobs] = useState<JobRecord[]>(AnalyticsService.getRecentJobs());
  const [filterTool, setFilterTool] = useState<string>('all');

  const refreshData = () => {
    setStats(AnalyticsService.getAggregatedStats());
    setJobs(AnalyticsService.getRecentJobs());
  };

  const handleUnlock = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (passcodeInput === ADMIN_PASSCODE) {
      sessionStorage.setItem(ADMIN_SESSION_KEY, 'true');
      setUnlocked(true);
      setPasscodeError(false);
    } else {
      setPasscodeError(true);
    }
  };

  const filteredJobs = jobs.filter((j) => (filterTool === 'all' ? true : j.toolSlug === filterTool));

  if (!unlocked) {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-md flex-col items-center px-4 py-24 text-center">
        <div className="mb-4 flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-indigo-500/10 text-indigo-400">
          <Lock className="h-6 w-6" />
        </div>
        <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-white">Admin Access</h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-400">
          This console is restricted. Enter the passcode to continue.
        </p>
        <form onSubmit={handleUnlock} className="mt-6 w-full space-y-3">
          <input
            type="password"
            value={passcodeInput}
            onChange={(e) => {
              setPasscodeInput(e.target.value);
              setPasscodeError(false);
            }}
            placeholder="Passcode"
            autoFocus
            className={`w-full rounded-lg border bg-slate-900 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm text-white outline-none focus:border-indigo-500 ${
              passcodeError ? 'border-rose-500' : 'border-slate-700'
            }`}
          />
          {passcodeError && (
            <p className="text-left text-xs text-rose-400">Incorrect passcode. Try again.</p>
          )}
          <button
            type="submit"
            className="w-full rounded-lg bg-indigo-600 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-indigo-500"
          >
            Unlock
          </button>
        </form>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 py-10 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 space-y-8">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between gap-4 border-b border-slate-800 pb-6">
        <div>
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-slate-800 bg-slate-800/60 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-indigo-400 mb-2">
            <Activity className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            <span>Operational Console</span>
          </div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-extrabold tracking-tight text-white">
            System & Usage Analytics
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-400">
            Local processing history for this browser — DocuForge runs entirely client-side,
            so there's no server collecting usage across visitors.
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={refreshData}
          className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-800 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-700 hover:text-white transition-colors self-start sm:self-auto"
        >
          <RefreshCw className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> Refresh Telemetry
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Top Level Metric KPIs */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
        {/* KPI 1 */}
        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/60 p-5">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-slate-400">
            <span className="text-xs font-medium">Total Jobs Executed</span>
            <div className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-indigo-500/10 text-indigo-400">
              <Layers className="h-4 w-4" />
            </div>
          </div>
          <p className="mt-3 text-2xl font-bold text-white tracking-tight">
            {stats.totalJobs.toLocaleString()}
          </p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-[11px] text-slate-500 font-medium">
            this browser, all time
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* KPI 2 */}
        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/60 p-5">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-slate-400">
            <span className="text-xs font-medium">Success Rate</span>
            <div className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400">
              <CheckCircle2 className="h-4 w-4" />
            </div>
          </div>
          <p className="mt-3 text-2xl font-bold text-emerald-400 tracking-tight">
            {stats.successRate}%
          </p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-[11px] text-slate-400">
            Engine validation pass rate
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* KPI 3 */}
        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/60 p-5">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-slate-400">
            <span className="text-xs font-medium">Data Processed</span>
            <div className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-blue-500/10 text-blue-400">
              <BarChart3 className="h-4 w-4" />
            </div>
          </div>
          <p className="mt-3 text-2xl font-bold text-white tracking-tight">
            {formatBytes(stats.totalBytes)}
          </p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-[11px] text-slate-400">
            In-memory stream throughput
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* KPI 4 */}
        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/60 p-5">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-slate-400">
            <span className="text-xs font-medium">Cloud Storage Used</span>
            <div className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-indigo-500/10 text-indigo-400">
              <HardDrive className="h-4 w-4" />
            </div>
          </div>
          <p className="mt-3 text-2xl font-bold text-white tracking-tight">
            0.00 Bytes
          </p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-[11px] text-emerald-400 font-medium">
            Zero cloud retention policy
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Breakdown Section: Most-Used Tools & Engine Health */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 lg:grid-cols-3">
        {/* Most-Used Tools List */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-1 rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/60 p-5 space-y-4">
          <h2 className="text-sm font-bold text-white tracking-tight">
            Most-Used Document Tools
          </h2>
          <div className="space-y-3">
            {stats.toolsBreakdown.length > 0 ? (
              stats.toolsBreakdown.slice(0, 6).map((item, idx) => (
                <div key={item.toolId} className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
                    <span className="text-slate-500 font-mono">#{idx + 1}</span>
                    <span className="truncate font-medium text-slate-200">{item.toolName}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3 shrink-0">
                    <span className="font-semibold text-indigo-400">{item.count} runs</span>
                    <span className="text-slate-500">{formatBytes(item.bytesProcessed)}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))
            ) : (
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                No tools used yet in this browser. Usage will appear here as tools are run.
              </p>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Recent Jobs Audit Table */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-2 rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/60 p-5 space-y-4">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
            <h2 className="text-sm font-bold text-white tracking-tight">
              Recent Processing Jobs (Client Audit Log)
            </h2>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <select
                value={filterTool}
                onChange={(e) => setFilterTool(e.target.value)}
                className="rounded-lg border border-slate-700 bg-slate-800 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-200 focus:border-indigo-500 focus:outline-none"
              >
                <option value="all">All tools</option>
                {Array.from(new Map(jobs.map((j) => [j.toolSlug, j.toolName])).entries()).map(
                  ([slug, name]) => (
                    <option key={slug} value={slug}>
                      {name}
                    </option>
                  )
                )}
              </select>
              <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 whitespace-nowrap">
                Showing last {filteredJobs.length} events
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            {filteredJobs.length === 0 ? (
              <p className="py-6 text-center text-xs text-slate-500">
                {jobs.length === 0
                  ? 'No processing jobs recorded yet in this browser.'
                  : 'No jobs match the selected tool filter.'}
              </p>
            ) : (
            <table className="w-full text-left text-xs">
              <thead className="border-b border-slate-800 text-slate-400">
                <tr>
                  <th className="pb-2 font-medium">Job ID</th>
                  <th className="pb-2 font-medium">Tool</th>
                  <th className="pb-2 font-medium">Size</th>
                  <th className="pb-2 font-medium">Duration</th>
                  <th className="pb-2 font-medium">Status</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-800/60 text-slate-300">
                {filteredJobs.map((job) => (
                  <tr key={job.id} className="hover:bg-slate-800/40">
                    <td className="py-2.5 font-mono text-[11px] text-slate-400">{job.id}</td>
                    <td className="py-2.5 font-medium text-white">{job.toolName}</td>
                    <td className="py-2.5 font-mono text-[11px]">
                      {formatBytes(job.originalSize)}
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-2.5 font-mono text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                      {job.durationMs}ms
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-2.5">
                      <span
                        className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold ${
                          job.status === 'completed'
                            ? 'bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400'
                            : 'bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-400'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {job.status === 'completed' ? 'Completed' : 'Failed'}
                      </span>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

FILE: src/pages/BlogPage.tsx

import React from 'react';
import { BookOpen, Calendar, Clock, User, ArrowRight, ArrowLeft } from 'lucide-react';
import { BLOG_POSTS } from '../config/blog';

interface BlogPageProps {
  currentSlug?: string;
  onNavigate: (path: string) => void;
}

export const BlogPage: React.FC<BlogPageProps> = ({ currentSlug, onNavigate }) => {
  // If viewing a specific article
  const activePost = BLOG_POSTS.find((p) => p.slug === currentSlug);

  if (activePost) {
    return (
      <article className="mx-auto max-w-3xl px-4 py-10 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 space-y-8">
        <button
          onClick={() => onNavigate('/blog')}
          className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-400 hover:text-white transition-colors"
        >
          <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" /> Back to Guides & Articles
        </button>

        <header className="space-y-4 border-b border-slate-800 pb-8">
          <span className="rounded-full bg-indigo-500/10 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-indigo-400 border border-indigo-500/20">
            {activePost.category}
          </span>

          <h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-4xl font-extrabold tracking-tight text-white leading-tight">
            {activePost.title}
          </h1>

          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4 text-xs text-slate-400">
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <User className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> {activePost.author}
            </span>
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <Calendar className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> {activePost.date}
            </span>
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <Clock className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> {activePost.readTime}
            </span>
          </div>
        </header>

        {/* Article Body */}
        <div className="space-y-5 text-sm sm:text-base leading-relaxed text-slate-300">
          {activePost.content.map((p, idx) => (
            <p key={idx}>{p}</p>
          ))}
        </div>

        {/* Tool Action Box */}
        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-indigo-500/30 bg-indigo-600/10 p-6 flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4">
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-white">Try the Free Online Tool</h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
              Put this guide into practice with zero software download.
            </p>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={() => onNavigate(`/${activePost.relatedToolSlug}`)}
            className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 px-5 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold text-white hover:bg-indigo-500 transition-colors shrink-0"
          >
            Launch Tool <ArrowRight className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
          </button>
        </div>
      </article>
    );
  }

  // Blog Directory Listing
  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 py-12 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 space-y-10">
      <div className="max-w-2xl">
        <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-slate-800 bg-slate-800/60 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-indigo-400 mb-3">
          <BookOpen className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
          <span>Document Knowledge Base</span>
        </div>
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-extrabold tracking-tight text-white sm:text-4xl">
          Guides, Tutorials & Best Practices
        </h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-400">
          Master PDF compression, document privacy, font optimization, and file conversion standards.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 md:grid-cols-2">
        {BLOG_POSTS.map((post) => (
          <div
            key={post.slug}
            onClick={() => onNavigate(`/blog/${post.slug}`)}
            className="group flex flex-col justify-between rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/60 p-6 transition-all hover:border-indigo-500/50 hover:bg-slate-800/80 cursor-pointer"
          >
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-slate-400 mb-3">
                <span className="font-semibold text-indigo-400">{post.category}</span>
                <span>{post.readTime}</span>
              </div>
              <h2 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold text-white group-hover:text-indigo-400 transition-colors">
                {post.title}
              </h2>
              <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
                {post.description}
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="mt-6 flex items-center justify-between border-t border-slate-800/60 pt-4 text-xs">
              <span className="text-slate-400">{post.date}</span>
              <span className="flex items-center gap-1 font-semibold text-indigo-400 group-hover:translate-x-1 transition-transform">
                Read Article <ArrowRight className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Mail, MessageSquare, Send, CheckCircle2, Bug, Lightbulb, HelpCircle } from 'lucide-react';

// TODO: replace with the real support inbox before launch — this is currently
// a placeholder and mail sent via the mailto: link below will not reach anyone.
const SUPPORT_EMAIL = 'support@docuforge.app';

const CATEGORY_LABEL: Record<'general' | 'bug' | 'feature', string> = {
  general: 'General question',
  bug: 'Bug report',
  feature: 'Feature idea'
};

export const ContactPage: React.FC = () => {
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [category, setCategory] = useState<'general' | 'bug' | 'feature'>('general');
  const [message, setMessage] = useState('');
  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false);

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!name || !email || !message) return;

    // No backend exists to receive this form, so the only honest way to
    // actually deliver the message is to hand it to the person's own email
    // client via a mailto: link — nothing is silently "sent" server-side.
    const subject = `[DocuForge — ${CATEGORY_LABEL[category]}] from ${name}`;
    const body = `${message}\n\n—\nFrom: ${name} <${email}>`;
    const mailtoUrl = `mailto:${SUPPORT_EMAIL}?subject=${encodeURIComponent(subject)}&body=${encodeURIComponent(body)}`;
    window.location.href = mailtoUrl;

    setSubmitted(true);
  };

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl px-4 py-12 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 space-y-8">
      <header className="text-center space-y-3">
        <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-slate-800 bg-slate-800/60 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-indigo-400">
          <MessageSquare className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
          <span>Support & Feedback</span>
        </div>
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-extrabold tracking-tight text-white sm:text-4xl">
          Get in Touch
        </h1>
        <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-400 max-w-lg mx-auto">
          Have a question about document processing, found a bug with a specific PDF format, or want to suggest a new tool? We'd love to hear from you.
        </p>
      </header>

      <div className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/80 p-6 sm:p-10 shadow-xl">
        {submitted ? (
          <div className="text-center py-8 space-y-4">
            <div className="flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400 border border-emerald-500/20 mx-auto">
              <CheckCircle2 className="h-7 w-7" />
            </div>
            <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-white">Almost done!</h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-300 max-w-sm mx-auto">
              Your email app should be opening now with your message pre-filled to{' '}
              <span className="font-mono text-slate-200">{SUPPORT_EMAIL}</span>. Just hit send from there —
              nothing is transmitted until you do.
            </p>
            <button
              onClick={() => {
                setSubmitted(false);
                setMessage('');
              }}
              className="mt-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-800 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-700"
            >
              Send Another Note
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-5">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2">
              <div>
                <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300">Your Name</label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  required
                  value={name}
                  onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="e.g. Alex Rivera"
                  className="mt-1 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs text-white placeholder-slate-500 outline-none focus:border-indigo-500"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300">Your Email</label>
                <input
                  type="email"
                  required
                  value={email}
                  onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="alex@example.com"
                  className="mt-1 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs text-white placeholder-slate-500 outline-none focus:border-indigo-500"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300">Subject Category</label>
              <div className="mt-2 grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setCategory('general')}
                  className={`flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border p-2.5 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors ${
                    category === 'general'
                      ? 'border-indigo-500 bg-indigo-500/10 text-white'
                      : 'border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 text-slate-400 hover:border-slate-700'
                  }`}
                >
                  <HelpCircle className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> General
                </button>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setCategory('bug')}
                  className={`flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border p-2.5 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors ${
                    category === 'bug'
                      ? 'border-rose-500 bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-300'
                      : 'border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 text-slate-400 hover:border-slate-700'
                  }`}
                >
                  <Bug className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> Bug Report
                </button>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setCategory('feature')}
                  className={`flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border p-2.5 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors ${
                    category === 'feature'
                      ? 'border-emerald-500 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-300'
                      : 'border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 text-slate-400 hover:border-slate-700'
                  }`}
                >
                  <Lightbulb className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> Feature Idea
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300">Message</label>
              <textarea
                rows={5}
                required
                value={message}
                onChange={(e) => setMessage(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Describe your question, request, or issue in detail..."
                className="mt-1 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 p-3.5 text-xs text-white placeholder-slate-500 outline-none focus:border-indigo-500"
              />
            </div>

            <button
              type="submit"
              className="flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 px-6 py-3.5 text-xs font-bold text-white shadow-lg shadow-indigo-600/25 transition-all hover:bg-indigo-500 active:scale-[0.99]"
            >
              <Send className="h-4 w-4" /> Send Message
            </button>
          </form>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between text-xs text-slate-400 px-2 gap-2">
        <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <Mail className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-indigo-400" />
          Direct Inquiries: <span className="text-slate-300 font-mono">{SUPPORT_EMAIL}</span>
        </span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

                  <span>Protected against malicious scripts and executable exploits</span>
                </li>
              </ul>
            </div>

                <span className="text-emerald-400">CLIENT-SIDE</span>
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-1 text-slate-300 text-[11px]">
                <p className="text-indigo-400">&gt; Your file content: never uploaded or transmitted</p>
                <p className="text-indigo-400">&gt; Processing location: your browser's memory only</p>
                <p className="text-indigo-400">&gt; Engine assets (fonts, OCR model): may load from a CDN on first use</p>
                <p className="text-slate-400">&gt; In-memory data: cleared when you close or refresh the tab</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
};

FILE: src/pages/LegalPage.tsx

import React from 'react';
import { Shield } from 'lucide-react';

interface LegalPageProps {
  type: 'privacy' | 'terms' | 'security';
  onNavigate: (path: string) => void;
}

export const LegalPage: React.FC<LegalPageProps> = ({ type, onNavigate }) => {
  const titles = {
    privacy: 'Privacy Policy',
    terms: 'Terms of Service',
    security: 'Security Architecture'
  };

  const tabs: { key: typeof type; label: string; path: string }[] = [
    { key: 'privacy', label: 'Privacy Policy', path: '/privacy' },
    { key: 'terms', label: 'Terms of Service', path: '/terms' },
    { key: 'security', label: 'Security', path: '/security' }
  ];

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl px-4 py-12 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 space-y-8">
      <header className="border-b border-slate-800 pb-6">
        <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-slate-800 bg-slate-800/60 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-indigo-400 mb-3">
          <Shield className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
          <span>Legal & Trust Center</span>
        </div>
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-extrabold tracking-tight text-white sm:text-4xl">
          {titles[type]}
        </h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-400">
          Last Updated: February 2026 &bull; Production Architecture Verification
        </p>

        <nav className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {tabs.map((tab) => (
            <button
              key={tab.key}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onNavigate(tab.path)}
              className={`rounded-lg px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors ${
                tab.key === type
                  ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white'
                  : 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-700 hover:text-white'
              }`}
            >
              {tab.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </nav>
      </header>

      {type === 'privacy' && (
        <div className="space-y-6 text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-300 leading-relaxed">
          <section className="space-y-3">
            <h2 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold text-white">1. Real Privacy Guarantee</h2>
            <p>
              DocuForge is built with privacy-first client execution. When you use tools such as Merge PDF, Split PDF, Compress PDF, Rotate PDF, Delete Pages, Image Converter, and Word Counter, your documents are processed directly within your browser’s local memory sandbox.
            </p>
            <p>
              Your confidential legal agreements, invoices, medical records, and bank statements are <strong>never uploaded to or stored on remote file servers</strong>.
            </p>
          </section>

          <section className="space-y-3">
            <h2 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold text-white">2. Automatic Session Cleanup</h2>
            <p>
              All intermediate binary blobs, rendered thumbnail canvases, and extracted text buffers are stored in transient browser memory (Blob URLs). When you download your file, refresh your browser, or close the browser tab, the operating system releases all occupied memory automatically.
            </p>
          </section>

          <section className="space-y-3">
            <h2 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold text-white">3. No Server-Side Telemetry</h2>
            <p>
              DocuForge has no backend server, so there is nothing to send usage data to. The "System & Usage Analytics"
              dashboard you may see in this app reads a small processing log stored only in your own browser's local
              storage on your device — it is never transmitted anywhere, never aggregated across visitors, and is
              cleared if you clear your browser data.
            </p>
          </section>
        </div>
      )}

      {type === 'security' && (
        <div className="space-y-6 text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-300 leading-relaxed">
          <section className="space-y-3">
            <h2 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold text-white">1. Threat Model & Sandboxing</h2>
            <p>
              Processing documents online carries intrinsic risks if executed carelessly. DocuForge enforces a multi-tier defense:
            </p>
            <ul className="list-disc pl-5 space-y-1.5 text-slate-300">
              <li><strong>Zero Execution Environment:</strong> Uploaded files cannot trigger arbitrary scripts or executable commands. Extensions like <code>.exe</code>, <code>.bat</code>, <code>.sh</code>, <code>.php</code>, and <code>.vbs</code> are strictly denied before processing.</li>
              <li><strong>MIME Header & Magic-Byte Validation:</strong> Beyond the file extension, the actual header bytes of uploaded PDFs, images, and Office files are checked against their real file signatures to catch disguised or mismatched files before processing.</li>
              <li><strong>Sandboxed Execution:</strong> Processing runs inside the browser's own script sandbox with standard origin security — WebAssembly is used specifically for the OCR engine, with HTML5 Canvas used for page rendering and image tools.</li>
            </ul>
          </section>

          <section className="space-y-3">
            <h2 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold text-white">2. SSL / HTTPS Transport</h2>
            <p>
              All assets and code bundles are delivered over HTTPS. The exact TLS version and HSTS configuration are enforced at the hosting/CDN layer rather than by this application itself, consistent with standard practice for static web apps.
            </p>
          </section>
        </div>
      )}

      {type === 'terms' && (
        <div className="space-y-6 text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-300 leading-relaxed">
          <section className="space-y-3">
            <h2 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold text-white">1. Permitted Use</h2>
            <p>
              DocuForge is provided for personal, academic, and business document management. You retain full ownership and intellectual property rights to all files you process through the service.
            </p>
          </section>

          <section className="space-y-3">
            <h2 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold text-white">2. Prohibited Uses</h2>
            <p>
              You agree not to use DocuForge to process malicious software, botnet payloads, or content that infringes upon the intellectual property or legal rights of third parties.
            </p>
          </section>

          <section className="space-y-3">
            <h2 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold text-white">3. Disclaimer of Warranty</h2>
            <p>
              While our processing algorithms undergo rigorous verification against PDF specifications, DocuForge is provided "as is". We always recommend keeping an original copy of critical legal or financial paperwork.
            </p>
          </section>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

FILE: src/pages/ToolDirectoryPage.tsx
======================================================================
import React, { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import { Search, ArrowRight } from 'lucide-react';
import { TOOLS_CONFIG, CATEGORIES_CONFIG } from '../config/tools';
import { ToolCategory } from '../types';
import { getToolIcon } from '../utils/toolIcon';

interface ToolDirectoryPageProps {
  initialCategory?: ToolCategory | 'all';
  onNavigate: (path: string) => void;
}

export const ToolDirectoryPage: React.FC<ToolDirectoryPageProps> = ({
  initialCategory = 'all',
  onNavigate
}) => {
  const [selectedCategory, setSelectedCategory] = useState<string>(initialCategory);
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');

  const filteredTools = useMemo(() => {
    return TOOLS_CONFIG.filter((tool) => {
      const matchesCategory = selectedCategory === 'all' || tool.category === selectedCategory;
      const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase().trim();
      const matchesSearch =
        !q ||
        tool.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        tool.shortDescription.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        tool.acceptedFileTypes.some((ext) => ext.toLowerCase().includes(q));

      return matchesCategory && matchesSearch;
    });
  }, [selectedCategory, searchQuery]);

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 py-10 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 space-y-8">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="max-w-2xl">
        <h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-extrabold tracking-tight text-white">
          All Document Tools
        </h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-400">
          Browse our complete catalog of {TOOLS_CONFIG.length} free, fast, and private PDF and document utilities.
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Filter and Search Bar */}
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between border-b border-slate-800 pb-6">
        {/* Category Pills */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
          <button
            onClick={() => setSelectedCategory('all')}
            className={`rounded-lg px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors ${
              selectedCategory === 'all'
                ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white'
                : 'bg-slate-800/80 text-slate-400 hover:bg-slate-800 hover:text-white'
            }`}
          >
            All ({TOOLS_CONFIG.length})
          </button>

          {CATEGORIES_CONFIG.map((cat) => {
            const count = TOOLS_CONFIG.filter((t) => t.category === cat.id).length;
            const isSelected = selectedCategory === cat.id;
            return (
              <button
                key={cat.id}
                onClick={() => setSelectedCategory(cat.id)}
                className={`rounded-lg px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors ${
                  isSelected
                    ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white'
                    : 'bg-slate-800/80 text-slate-400 hover:bg-slate-800 hover:text-white'
                }`}
              >
                {cat.name} ({count})
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        {/* Search input */}
        <div className="relative w-full sm:w-72">
          <Search className="absolute left-3 top-2.5 h-4 w-4 text-slate-500" />
          <input
            type="text"
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Search tools..."
            className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-800/80 py-2 pl-9 pr-4 text-xs text-white placeholder-slate-500 outline-none focus:border-indigo-500"
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Grid of Tools */}
      {filteredTools.length > 0 ? (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
          {filteredTools.map((tool) => {
            const Icon = getToolIcon(tool.iconName);
            return (
              <div
                key={tool.id}
                onClick={() => onNavigate(`/${tool.slug}`)}
                className="group flex flex-col justify-between rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/60 p-5 transition-all hover:border-indigo-500/50 hover:bg-slate-800/80 hover:shadow-lg hover:shadow-indigo-500/5 cursor-pointer"
              >
                <div>
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
                    <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-indigo-500/10 text-indigo-400 group-hover:bg-indigo-600 group-hover:text-white transition-colors">
                      <Icon className="h-5 w-5" />
                    </div>
                    <span className="rounded-full bg-slate-800 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 border border-slate-700/60">
                      {tool.category}
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-white group-hover:text-indigo-400 transition-colors">
                    {tool.name}
                  </h3>
                  <p className="mt-1.5 text-xs text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
                    {tool.shortDescription}
                  </p>
                </div>

                <div className="mt-4 flex items-center justify-between border-t border-slate-800/60 pt-3 text-[11px]">
                  <span className="text-slate-400 truncate max-w-[160px]">
                    {tool.acceptedFileTypes.join(', ')}
                  </span>
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-1 font-semibold text-indigo-400 group-hover:translate-x-0.5 transition-transform">
                    Launch <ArrowRight className="h-3 w-3" />
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/40 p-12 text-center">
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-400">
            No tools found matching your current filter.
          </p>
          <button
            onClick={() => {
              setSelectedCategory('all');
              setSearchQuery('');
            }}
            className="mt-3 rounded-lg bg-indigo-600 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white hover:bg-indigo-500"
          >
            Clear Filters
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

======================================================================
FILE: src/pages/ToolPage.tsx
======================================================================
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import {
  AlertCircle,
  FileCheck,
  Eraser,
  Wrench,
  Unlock
} from 'lucide-react';
import { ToolDefinition, UploadedFileItem, ProcessingState, ProcessedResult, PDFPageInfo } from '../types';
import { FileUploader } from '../components/FileUploader';
import { ProgressBar } from '../components/ProgressBar';
import { ResultCard } from '../components/ResultCard';
import { PageThumbnailGrid } from '../components/PageThumbnailGrid';
import { FAQAccordion } from '../components/FAQAccordion';
import { RelatedTools } from '../components/RelatedTools';
import { PDFEngine, PDFPasswordError } from '../services/pdfEngine';
import { RenderEngine } from '../services/renderEngine';
import { ConversionEngine } from '../services/conversionEngine';
import { ImageEngine } from '../services/imageEngine';
import { OCREngine } from '../services/ocrEngine';
import { AnalyticsService } from '../services/analyticsService';
import { analyzeText, formatExactBytes } from '../utils/formatters';
import { getToolIcon } from '../utils/toolIcon';
import { categoryLabel } from '../config/seo';

interface ToolPageProps {
  tool: ToolDefinition;
  onNavigate: (path: string) => void;
}

export const ToolPage: React.FC<ToolPageProps> = ({ tool, onNavigate }) => {
  const [files, setFiles] = useState<UploadedFileItem[]>([]);
  const [processingState, setProcessingState] = useState<ProcessingState>('idle');
  const [progressPercent, setProgressPercent] = useState<number>(0);
  const [statusMessage, setStatusMessage] = useState<string>('');
  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [result, setResult] = useState<ProcessedResult | null>(null);

  // PDF page tools state (Rotate, Delete, Extract, Reorder)
  const [pdfPages, setPdfPages] = useState<PDFPageInfo[]>([]);
  const [loadingPages, setLoadingPages] = useState(false);

  // Specific Tool Options State
  // Split options
  const [splitMode, setSplitMode] = useState<'all_pages' | 'custom_ranges'>('all_pages');
  const [splitRange, setSplitRange] = useState('1-3');

  // Compress PDF options
  const [compressLevel, setCompressLevel] = useState<'high' | 'recommended' | 'low'>('recommended');

  // Image to PDF options
  const [imgPdfPageSize, setImgPdfPageSize] = useState<'a4' | 'letter' | 'fit'>('a4');
  const [imgPdfOrientation, setImgPdfOrientation] = useState<'portrait' | 'landscape'>('portrait');
  const [imgPdfMargin, setImgPdfMargin] = useState<'none' | 'small' | 'large'>('small');

  // Compress Image options
  const [imgQuality, setImgQuality] = useState<number>(0.75);
  const [imgFormat, setImgFormat] = useState<'image/jpeg' | 'image/png' | 'image/webp'>('image/jpeg');

  // Resize Image options
  const [imgWidth, setImgWidth] = useState<number>(1280);
  const [imgHeight, setImgHeight] = useState<number>(720);
  const [lockAspect, setLockAspect] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [aspectRatio, setAspectRatio] = useState<number>(16 / 9);

  // Utilities direct text input state
  const [utilityText, setUtilityText] = useState<string>('');

  // 1. Page Number Options
  const [pageNumPosition, setPageNumPosition] = useState<'bottom-center' | 'bottom-right' | 'bottom-left' | 'top-center' | 'top-right'>('bottom-center');
  const [pageNumFormat, setPageNumFormat] = useState<'page_x_of_y' | 'x_of_y' | 'x'>('page_x_of_y');
  const [pageNumStart, setPageNumStart] = useState<number>(1);

  // 2. Watermark Options
  const [wmType, setWmType] = useState<'text' | 'image'>('text');
  const [wmText, setWmText] = useState<string>('CONFIDENTIAL');
  const [wmOpacity, setWmOpacity] = useState<number>(0.25);
  const [wmRotation, setWmRotation] = useState<number>(45);
  const [wmColor, setWmColor] = useState<'gray' | 'red' | 'blue' | 'black'>('gray');
  const [wmImageFile, setWmImageFile] = useState<File | null>(null);

  // 3. Edit Metadata Options
  const [metaTitle, setMetaTitle] = useState<string>('');
  const [metaAuthor, setMetaAuthor] = useState<string>('');
  const [metaSubject, setMetaSubject] = useState<string>('');
  const [metaKeywords, setMetaKeywords] = useState<string>('');

  // 4. Resize PDF Pages Options
  const [targetPdfPageSize, setTargetPdfPageSize] = useState<'a4' | 'letter' | 'legal' | 'a3'>('a4');
  const [targetPdfOrientation, setTargetPdfOrientation] = useState<'portrait' | 'landscape' | 'match'>('portrait');

  // 5. Crop PDF Options (in points)
  const [cropTop, setCropTop] = useState<number>(36);
  const [cropBottom, setCropBottom] = useState<number>(36);
  const [cropLeft, setCropLeft] = useState<number>(36);
  const [cropRight, setCropRight] = useState<number>(36);

  // Unlock PDF: optional password for files that need one to open at all
  const [unlockPassword, setUnlockPassword] = useState<string>('');

  // 6. Sign PDF Options & Canvas
  const [sigPosition, setSigPosition] = useState<'bottom-right' | 'bottom-left' | 'bottom-center' | 'center'>('bottom-right');
  const [sigTargetPage, setSigTargetPage] = useState<'last' | 'first' | 'all'>('last');
  const [sigPenColor, setSigPenColor] = useState<'#000000' | '#1e3a8a'>('#1e3a8a');
  const [signatureDataUrl, setSignatureDataUrl] = useState<string>('');
  const [isDrawing, setIsDrawing] = useState(false);
  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement | null>(null);

  // Clean state when tool changes
  useEffect(() => {
    resetState();
  }, [tool.id]);

  const resetState = () => {
    setFiles([]);
    setProcessingState('idle');
    setProgressPercent(0);
    setStatusMessage('');
    setErrorMessage(null);
    setResult(null);
    setPdfPages([]);
    setLoadingPages(false);
    setSignatureDataUrl('');
    setWmImageFile(null);
    setUnlockPassword('');
    clearSignatureCanvas();
  };

  const clearSignatureCanvas = () => {
    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    if (canvas) {
      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
      if (ctx) ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
    }
    setSignatureDataUrl('');
  };

  // Canvas drawing handlers for signature
  const startDrawing = (e: React.MouseEvent<HTMLCanvasElement> | React.TouchEvent<HTMLCanvasElement>) => {
    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    if (!canvas) return;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    if (!ctx) return;
    setIsDrawing(true);
    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    const clientX = 'touches' in e ? e.touches[0].clientX : e.clientX;
    const clientY = 'touches' in e ? e.touches[0].clientY : e.clientY;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(clientX - rect.left, clientY - rect.top);
    ctx.strokeStyle = sigPenColor;
    ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
    ctx.lineCap = 'round';
    ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
  };

  const draw = (e: React.MouseEvent<HTMLCanvasElement> | React.TouchEvent<HTMLCanvasElement>) => {
    if (!isDrawing) return;
    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    if (!canvas) return;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    if (!ctx) return;
    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    const clientX = 'touches' in e ? e.touches[0].clientX : e.clientX;
    const clientY = 'touches' in e ? e.touches[0].clientY : e.clientY;
    ctx.lineTo(clientX - rect.left, clientY - rect.top);
    ctx.stroke();
  };

  const stopDrawing = () => {
    if (!isDrawing) return;
    setIsDrawing(false);
    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    if (canvas) {
      setSignatureDataUrl(canvas.toDataURL('image/png'));
    }
  };

  // Auto-populate metadata when file is selected for edit-pdf-metadata
  useEffect(() => {
    if (tool.id === 'edit-pdf-metadata' && files.length === 1) {
      PDFEngine.inspectPDF(files[0].file)
        .then((info) => {
          if (info.title) setMetaTitle(info.title);
          if (info.author) setMetaAuthor(info.author);
          if (info.subject) setMetaSubject(info.subject);
          if (info.keywords) setMetaKeywords(info.keywords);
        })
        .catch(() => {});
    }
  }, [files, tool.id]);

  // If user uploads PDF for Rotate, Delete, Extract, or Reorder, load thumbnails
  useEffect(() => {
    const isPageTool = ['rotate-pdf', 'delete-pdf-pages', 'extract-pdf-pages', 'reorder-pdf-pages'].includes(tool.id);
    if (isPageTool && files.length === 1) {
      loadPageThumbnails(files[0].file);
    } else {
      setPdfPages([]);
    }
  }, [files, tool.id]);

  // If user uploads an image for Resize, measure original dimensions
  useEffect(() => {
    if (tool.id === 'resize-image' && files.length === 1) {
      ImageEngine.loadImage(files[0].file).then((img) => {
        setImgWidth(img.naturalWidth);
        setImgHeight(img.naturalHeight);
        setAspectRatio(img.naturalWidth / img.naturalHeight);
      });
    }
  }, [files, tool.id]);

  const loadPageThumbnails = async (file: File) => {
    setLoadingPages(true);
    try {
      const inspect = await PDFEngine.inspectPDF(file);
      const thumbs = await RenderEngine.generatePageThumbnails(file, 40);

      const items: PDFPageInfo[] = Array.from({ length: inspect.pageCount }, (_, i) => {
        const pageNum = i + 1;
        const found = thumbs.find((t) => t.pageNumber === pageNum);
        return {
          pageNumber: pageNum,
          rotation: 0,
          selected: tool.id === 'extract-pdf-pages' ? i === 0 : false, // select first page by default for extract
          thumbnailUrl: found?.thumbnailUrl
        };
      });
      setPdfPages(items);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.warn('Failed to load page previews:', err);
    } finally {
      setLoadingPages(false);
    }
  };

  // Rotate handlers
  const handleRotateSinglePage = (pageNumber: number) => {
    setPdfPages((prev) =>
      prev.map((p) => (p.pageNumber === pageNumber ? { ...p, rotation: (p.rotation + 90) % 360 } : p))
    );
  };

  const handleRotateAll = () => {
    setPdfPages((prev) => prev.map((p) => ({ ...p, rotation: (p.rotation + 90) % 360 })));
  };

  // Selection handlers
  const handleToggleSelectPage = (pageNumber: number) => {
    setPdfPages((prev) =>
      prev.map((p) => (p.pageNumber === pageNumber ? { ...p, selected: !p.selected } : p))
    );
  };

  const handleSelectAllPages = () => {
    setPdfPages((prev) => prev.map((p) => ({ ...p, selected: true })));
  };

  const handleDeselectAllPages = () => {
    setPdfPages((prev) => prev.map((p) => ({ ...p, selected: false })));
  };

  // Reorder handlers
  const handleMovePage = (index: number, direction: 'left' | 'right') => {
    const target = direction === 'left' ? index - 1 : index + 1;
    if (target < 0 || target >= pdfPages.length) return;
    const copy = [...pdfPages];
    const [moved] = copy.splice(index, 1);
    copy.splice(target, 0, moved);
    setPdfPages(copy);
  };

  // Width/Height sync with aspect lock
  const handleWidthChange = (val: number) => {
    setImgWidth(val);
    if (lockAspect && aspectRatio > 0) {
      setImgHeight(Math.round(val / aspectRatio));
    }
  };

  const handleHeightChange = (val: number) => {
    setImgHeight(val);
    if (lockAspect && aspectRatio > 0) {
      setImgWidth(Math.round(val * aspectRatio));
    }
  };

  // Main Execution Process
  const handleExecuteProcessing = async () => {
    if (files.length === 0 && !['word-counter', 'character-counter'].includes(tool.id)) {
      setErrorMessage('Please select a file to process.');
      return;
    }

    setErrorMessage(null);
    setProcessingState('uploading');
    setProgressPercent(15);
    setStatusMessage('Reading file data...');

    const startTime = Date.now();
    const primaryFile = files[0]?.file;
    const totalOriginalSize = files.reduce((acc, f) => acc + f.size, 0);

    try {
      // Transition to analyzing
      setTimeout(() => {
        setProcessingState('analyzing');
        setProgressPercent(35);
        setStatusMessage('Validating and preparing document streams...');
      }, 200);

      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 400));
      setProcessingState('processing');
      setProgressPercent(60);
      setStatusMessage('Executing processing engine...');

      let finalBlob: Blob;
      let finalFileName: string = 'document.pdf';
      let extractedText: string | undefined = undefined;
      let newSize: number = 0;
      let reductionPercent: number | undefined = undefined;
      let effectiveOriginalSize: number = totalOriginalSize;
      let resultPageCount: number | undefined = undefined;
      let resultMetadata: Record<string, string | number> | undefined = undefined;

      switch (tool.id) {
        // --- PDF TOOLS ---
        case 'merge-pdf': {
          const rawFiles = files.map((f) => f.file);
          finalBlob = await PDFEngine.mergePDFs(rawFiles);
          finalFileName = 'merged-document.pdf';
          newSize = finalBlob.size;
          break;
        }

        case 'split-pdf': {
          const res = await PDFEngine.splitPDF(primaryFile, splitMode, splitRange);
          finalBlob = res.blob;
          finalFileName = res.fileName;
          newSize = finalBlob.size;
          break;
        }

        case 'compress-pdf': {
          const comp = await PDFEngine.compressPDF(primaryFile, compressLevel);
          finalBlob = comp.blob;
          finalFileName = `compressed-${primaryFile.name}`;
          newSize = comp.newSize;
          reductionPercent = comp.reductionPercentage;
          resultMetadata = {
            Method:
              comp.method === 'rasterized'
                ? 'Image-based (aggressive)'
                : comp.method === 'unchanged'
                ? 'No change needed'
                : 'Structural optimization',
            ...(comp.note ? { Note: comp.note } : {})
          };
          break;
        }

        case 'rotate-pdf': {
          const rotations: Record<number, number> = {};
          pdfPages.forEach((p) => {
            if (p.rotation > 0) rotations[p.pageNumber] = p.rotation;
          });
          finalBlob = await PDFEngine.rotatePDF(primaryFile, rotations);
          finalFileName = `rotated-${primaryFile.name}`;
          newSize = finalBlob.size;
          break;
        }

        case 'delete-pdf-pages': {
          const toDelete = pdfPages.filter((p) => p.selected).map((p) => p.pageNumber);
          if (toDelete.length === 0) {
            throw new Error('Please select at least one page to delete.');
          }
          finalBlob = await PDFEngine.deletePages(primaryFile, toDelete);
          finalFileName = `trimmed-${primaryFile.name}`;
          newSize = finalBlob.size;
          break;
        }

        case 'extract-pdf-pages': {
          const toExtract = pdfPages.filter((p) => p.selected).map((p) => p.pageNumber);
          if (toExtract.length === 0) {
            throw new Error('Please select at least one page to extract.');
          }
          finalBlob = await PDFEngine.extractPages(primaryFile, toExtract);
          finalFileName = `extracted-${primaryFile.name}`;
          newSize = finalBlob.size;
          break;
        }

        case 'reorder-pdf-pages': {
          const order = pdfPages.map((p) => p.pageNumber);
          finalBlob = await PDFEngine.reorderPages(primaryFile, order);
          finalFileName = `reordered-${primaryFile.name}`;
          newSize = finalBlob.size;
          break;
        }

        // --- ADVANCED PDF TOOLS ---
        case 'page-number-pdf': {
          finalBlob = await PDFEngine.addPageNumbers(primaryFile, {
            position: pageNumPosition,
            format: pageNumFormat,
            startNumber: pageNumStart
          });
          finalFileName = `numbered-${primaryFile.name}`;
          newSize = finalBlob.size;
          resultMetadata = {
            Position: pageNumPosition.replace('-', ' '),
            Format: pageNumFormat === 'page_x_of_y' ? 'Page X of Y' : pageNumFormat === 'x_of_y' ? 'X / Y' : 'X',
            'Start Number': pageNumStart
          };
          break;
        }

        case 'watermark-pdf': {
          if (wmType === 'text' && !wmText.trim()) {
            throw new Error('Please enter text for your watermark.');
          }
          if (wmType === 'image' && !wmImageFile) {
            throw new Error('Please select an image file to use as a watermark.');
          }
          const wmRes = await PDFEngine.addWatermark(primaryFile, {
            type: wmType,
            text: wmText,
            imageFile: wmImageFile || undefined,
            opacity: wmOpacity,
            rotation: wmRotation,
            color: wmColor
          });
          finalBlob = wmRes.blob;
          finalFileName = `watermarked-${primaryFile.name}`;
          newSize = finalBlob.size;
          resultMetadata = {
            Type: wmType === 'text' ? 'Text stamp' : 'Image logo watermark',
            ...(wmType === 'text' ? { Text: wmText } : {}),
            Opacity: `${Math.round(wmOpacity * 100)}%`,
            Rotation: `${wmRotation}°`,
            ...(wmRes.note ? { Note: wmRes.note } : {})
          };
          break;
        }

        case 'edit-pdf-metadata': {
          finalBlob = await PDFEngine.editMetadata(primaryFile, {
            title: metaTitle,
            author: metaAuthor,
            subject: metaSubject,
            keywords: metaKeywords
          });
          finalFileName = `metadata-${primaryFile.name}`;
          newSize = finalBlob.size;
          resultMetadata = {
            Title: metaTitle || '(Unset)',
            Author: metaAuthor || '(Unset)',
            Subject: metaSubject || '(Unset)',
            Keywords: metaKeywords || '(None)'
          };
          break;
        }

        case 'flatten-pdf': {
          const flatRes = await PDFEngine.flattenPDF(primaryFile);
          finalBlob = flatRes.blob;
          finalFileName = `flattened-${primaryFile.name}`;
          newSize = finalBlob.size;
          resultMetadata = {
            'Fields Flattened': flatRes.fieldsFlattened,
            Status:
              flatRes.note || 'All interactive annotations and form fields converted to immutable vector paths'
          };
          break;
        }

        case 'sign-pdf': {
          if (!signatureDataUrl) {
            throw new Error('Please draw your signature or select an ink stroke before signing.');
          }
          finalBlob = await PDFEngine.signPDF(primaryFile, signatureDataUrl, {
            targetPage: sigTargetPage,
            position: sigPosition
          });
          finalFileName = `signed-${primaryFile.name}`;
          newSize = finalBlob.size;
          resultMetadata = {
            'Signed Pages': sigTargetPage === 'last' ? 'Last page' : sigTargetPage === 'first' ? 'First page' : 'All pages',
            Placement: sigPosition.replace('-', ' ')
          };
          break;
        }

        case 'resize-pdf-pages': {
          finalBlob = await PDFEngine.resizePDFPages(primaryFile, targetPdfPageSize, targetPdfOrientation);
          finalFileName = `resized-${targetPdfPageSize}-${primaryFile.name}`;
          newSize = finalBlob.size;
          resultMetadata = {
            'Target Dimensions': targetPdfPageSize.toUpperCase(),
            Orientation: targetPdfOrientation
          };
          break;
        }

        case 'crop-pdf': {
          finalBlob = await PDFEngine.cropPDF(primaryFile, {
            top: cropTop,
            bottom: cropBottom,
            left: cropLeft,
            right: cropRight
          });
          finalFileName = `cropped-${primaryFile.name}`;
          newSize = finalBlob.size;
          resultMetadata = {
            'Trim Insets': `Top: ${cropTop}pt, Bottom: ${cropBottom}pt, Left: ${cropLeft}pt, Right: ${cropRight}pt`
          };
          break;
        }

        case 'repair-pdf': {
          const repRes = await PDFEngine.repairPDF(primaryFile);
          finalBlob = repRes.blob;
          finalFileName = `repaired-${primaryFile.name}`;
          newSize = finalBlob.size;
          resultMetadata = {
            'Salvaged Pages': repRes.salvagedPages,
            Diagnostics: repRes.status
          };
          break;
        }

        case 'unlock-pdf': {
          const unlockRes = await PDFEngine.unlockPDF(primaryFile, unlockPassword.trim() || undefined);
          finalBlob = unlockRes.blob;
          finalFileName = `unlocked-${primaryFile.name}`;
          newSize = finalBlob.size;
          resultPageCount = unlockRes.method === 'rasterized' ? unlockRes.pageCount : undefined;
          resultMetadata = { Status: unlockRes.note };
          break;
        }

        // --- CONVERTERS ---
        case 'pdf-to-jpg': {
          const res = await RenderEngine.convertPDFToImages(primaryFile, 'image/jpeg');
          finalBlob = res.blob;
          finalFileName = res.fileName;
          newSize = finalBlob.size;
          break;
        }

        case 'pdf-to-png': {
          const res = await RenderEngine.convertPDFToImages(primaryFile, 'image/png');
          finalBlob = res.blob;
          finalFileName = res.fileName;
          newSize = finalBlob.size;
          break;
        }

        case 'jpg-to-pdf':
        case 'png-to-pdf':
        case 'image-to-pdf': {
          const rawImgs = files.map((f) => f.file);
          finalBlob = await PDFEngine.imagesToPDF(rawImgs, {
            pageSize: imgPdfPageSize,
            orientation: imgPdfOrientation,
            margin: imgPdfMargin
          });
          finalFileName = 'converted-images.pdf';
          newSize = finalBlob.size;
          break;
        }

        case 'pdf-to-word': {
          const wordRes = await ConversionEngine.pdfToWord(primaryFile);
          finalBlob = wordRes.blob;
          finalFileName = wordRes.fileName;
          extractedText = wordRes.textPreview;
          newSize = finalBlob.size;
          break;
        }

        case 'word-to-pdf': {
          finalBlob = await ConversionEngine.textToPDF(primaryFile);
          finalFileName = `${primaryFile.name.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, '')}.pdf`;
          newSize = finalBlob.size;
          break;
        }

        case 'pdf-to-excel': {
          const excelRes = await ConversionEngine.pdfToExcel(primaryFile);
          finalBlob = excelRes.blob;
          finalFileName = excelRes.fileName;
          newSize = finalBlob.size;
          break;
        }

        case 'excel-to-pdf': {
          finalBlob = await ConversionEngine.excelToPDF(primaryFile);
          finalFileName = `${primaryFile.name.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, '')}.pdf`;
          newSize = finalBlob.size;
          break;
        }

        case 'powerpoint-to-pdf': {
          finalBlob = await ConversionEngine.presentationToPDF(primaryFile);
          finalFileName = `${primaryFile.name.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, '')}-slides.pdf`;
          newSize = finalBlob.size;
          break;
        }

        case 'pdf-to-powerpoint': {
          const pptRes = await ConversionEngine.pdfToPowerPoint(primaryFile);
          finalBlob = pptRes.blob;
          finalFileName = pptRes.fileName;
          newSize = finalBlob.size;
          break;
        }

        // --- IMAGE TOOLS ---
        case 'compress-image': {
          const compImg = await ImageEngine.compressImage(primaryFile, imgQuality, imgFormat);
          finalBlob = compImg.blob;
          finalFileName = `compressed-${primaryFile.name.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, '')}.${compImg.format}`;
          newSize = compImg.newSize;
          reductionPercent = compImg.reductionPercentage;
          break;
        }

        case 'resize-image': {
          const resized = await ImageEngine.resizeImage(primaryFile, imgWidth, imgHeight, imgFormat);
          finalBlob = resized.blob;
          finalFileName = `resized-${imgWidth}x${imgHeight}-${primaryFile.name}`;
          newSize = resized.newSize;
          break;
        }

        case 'jpg-to-png': {
          const c = await ImageEngine.convertFormat(primaryFile, 'image/png');
          finalBlob = c.blob;
          finalFileName = `${primaryFile.name.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, '')}.png`;
          newSize = c.newSize;
          break;
        }

        case 'png-to-jpg':
        case 'webp-to-jpg': {
          const c = await ImageEngine.convertFormat(primaryFile, 'image/jpeg');
          finalBlob = c.blob;
          finalFileName = `${primaryFile.name.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, '')}.jpg`;
          newSize = c.newSize;
          break;
        }

        case 'jpg-to-webp':
        case 'png-to-webp': {
          const c = await ImageEngine.convertFormat(primaryFile, 'image/webp');
          finalBlob = c.blob;
          finalFileName = `${primaryFile.name.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, '')}.webp`;
          newSize = c.newSize;
          reductionPercent = c.reductionPercentage;
          break;
        }

        // --- OCR TOOLS ---
        case 'image-to-text': {
          setStatusMessage('Running character recognition...');
          const ocr = await OCREngine.recognizeImage(primaryFile, (p, msg) => {
            setProgressPercent(Math.round(p * 100));
            setStatusMessage(msg);
          });
          finalBlob = ocr.txtBlob;
          finalFileName = `${primaryFile.name.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, '')}-ocr.txt`;
          extractedText = ocr.text;
          newSize = finalBlob.size;
          break;
        }

        case 'pdf-to-text':
        case 'text-extractor': {
          setStatusMessage('Decoding text streams...');
          const ext = await OCREngine.extractPDFText(primaryFile, (curr, tot) => {
            setProgressPercent(Math.round((curr / tot) * 90));
          });
          finalBlob = ext.txtBlob;
          finalFileName = `${primaryFile.name.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, '')}-text.txt`;
          extractedText = ext.text;
          newSize = finalBlob.size;
          break;
        }

        case 'scanned-pdf-ocr': {
          setStatusMessage('Running OCR on scanned pages...');
          const scanned = await OCREngine.recognizeScannedPDF(primaryFile, (p, msg) => {
            setProgressPercent(Math.round(p * 100));
            setStatusMessage(msg);
          });
          finalBlob = scanned.txtBlob;
          finalFileName = `${primaryFile.name.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, '')}-scanned-ocr.txt`;
          extractedText = scanned.text;
          newSize = finalBlob.size;
          break;
        }

        // --- UTILITY TOOLS ---
        case 'word-counter':
        case 'character-counter': {
          const stats = analyzeText(utilityText);
          effectiveOriginalSize = new Blob([utilityText]).size;

          const reportLines = [
            `${tool.name} Report`,
            `Generated: ${new Date().toLocaleString()}`,
            '',
            `Words: ${stats.words}`,
            `Characters (with spaces): ${stats.characters}`,
            `Characters (no spaces): ${stats.charactersNoSpaces}`,
            `Sentences: ${stats.sentences}`,
            `Paragraphs: ${stats.paragraphs}`,
            `Estimated reading time: ${stats.readingTime}`,
            `Estimated speaking time: ${stats.speakingTime}`,
            '',
            stats.topKeywords.length > 0
              ? `Top keywords: ${stats.topKeywords.map((k) => `${k.word} (${k.count}, ${k.percentage}%)`).join(', ')}`
              : '',
            '',
            '--- Original Text ---',
            utilityText
          ].filter((l) => l !== undefined);

          finalBlob = new Blob([reportLines.join('\n')], { type: 'text/plain;charset=utf-8;' });
          finalFileName = `${tool.id}-report.txt`;
          newSize = finalBlob.size;
          extractedText = reportLines.slice(0, 10).join('\n');
          resultMetadata = {
            Words: stats.words,
            Characters: stats.characters,
            Sentences: stats.sentences,
            'Reading time': stats.readingTime
          };
          break;
        }

        case 'pdf-page-counter': {
          const info = await PDFEngine.inspectPDF(primaryFile);
          resultPageCount = info.pageCount;
          resultMetadata = {
            Pages: info.pageCount,
            ...(info.title ? { Title: info.title } : {}),
            ...(info.author ? { Author: info.author } : {}),
            ...(info.creator ? { Creator: info.creator } : {}),
            ...(info.producer ? { Producer: info.producer } : {}),
            ...(info.creationDate ? { Created: info.creationDate } : {}),
            ...(info.dimensions ? { Dimensions: `${info.dimensions.width} x ${info.dimensions.height} pt` } : {}),
            ...(info.pdfVersion ? { 'PDF Version': info.pdfVersion } : {}),
            ...(info.keywords ? { Keywords: info.keywords } : {}),
            Encrypted: info.encrypted ? 'Yes (some metadata is hidden)' : 'No'
          };

          const reportLines = [
            `PDF Page Count & Metadata Report`,
            `File: ${primaryFile.name}`,
            '',
            ...Object.entries(resultMetadata).map(([k, v]) => `${k}: ${v}`)
          ];

          finalBlob = new Blob([reportLines.join('\n')], { type: 'text/plain;charset=utf-8;' });
          finalFileName = `${primaryFile.name.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, '')}-page-info.txt`;
          newSize = finalBlob.size;
          extractedText = reportLines.join('\n');
          break;
        }

        case 'file-size-checker': {
          const bytes = primaryFile.size;
          resultMetadata = {
            'Exact bytes': formatExactBytes(bytes),
            Kilobytes: `${(bytes / 1024).toFixed(2)} KB`,
            Megabytes: `${(bytes / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(3)} MB`,
            'Gmail limit (25MB)': bytes <= 25 * 1024 * 1024 ? 'Within limit' : 'Exceeds limit',
            'Outlook limit (20MB)': bytes <= 20 * 1024 * 1024 ? 'Within limit' : 'Exceeds limit'
          };

          const reportLines = [
            `File Size Report`,
            `File: ${primaryFile.name}`,
            '',
            ...Object.entries(resultMetadata).map(([k, v]) => `${k}: ${v}`)
          ];

          finalBlob = new Blob([reportLines.join('\n')], { type: 'text/plain;charset=utf-8;' });
          finalFileName = `${primaryFile.name.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, '')}-size-report.txt`;
          newSize = finalBlob.size;
          extractedText = reportLines.join('\n');
          break;
        }

        default: {
          // Fallback generic PDF copy
          finalBlob = new Blob([await primaryFile.arrayBuffer()], { type: primaryFile.type });
          finalFileName = primaryFile.name;
          newSize = finalBlob.size;
        }
      }

      setProcessingState('finalizing');
      setProgressPercent(95);
      setStatusMessage('Generating secure download package...');

      const downloadUrl = URL.createObjectURL(finalBlob);

      setTimeout(() => {
        setProcessingState('completed');
        setProgressPercent(100);
        setStatusMessage('Complete!');

        setResult({
          fileName: finalFileName,
          blob: finalBlob,
          downloadUrl,
          originalSize: effectiveOriginalSize,
          newSize,
          reductionPercentage: reductionPercent,
          extractedText,
          pageCount: resultPageCount,
          metadata: resultMetadata
        });

        // Track job in anonymous audit analytics
        AnalyticsService.trackJob({
          toolSlug: tool.slug,
          toolName: tool.name,
          originalSize: effectiveOriginalSize,
          processedSize: newSize,
          durationMs: Date.now() - startTime,
          status: 'completed'
        });
      }, 300);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Processing error:', err);
      setProcessingState('error');
      const message =
        err instanceof PDFPasswordError
          ? tool.id === 'unlock-pdf'
            ? err.message + ' Enter it in the password field above and try again.'
            : err.message + ' This tool cannot prompt for a password — use Unlock PDF first, then re-upload the result here.'
          : err.message || 'We could not process this file. Please make sure the file is valid and try again.';
      setErrorMessage(message);

      AnalyticsService.trackJob({
        toolSlug: tool.slug,
        toolName: tool.name,
        originalSize: totalOriginalSize,
        processedSize: 0,
        durationMs: Date.now() - startTime,
        status: 'failed',
        errorMessage: err.message
      });
    }
  };

  const Icon = getToolIcon(tool.iconName);

  // Text analysis for word-counter and character-counter
  const textStats = analyzeText(utilityText);

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 space-y-10">
      {/* Breadcrumb */}
      <nav aria-label="Breadcrumb" className="text-xs text-slate-500">
        <ol className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
          <li>
            <button onClick={() => onNavigate('/')} className="hover:text-slate-300 transition-colors">
              Home
            </button>
          </li>
          <li aria-hidden="true">/</li>
          <li>
            <button
              onClick={() => onNavigate(`/tools?cat=${tool.category}`)}
              className="capitalize hover:text-slate-300 transition-colors"
            >
              {categoryLabel(tool.category)}
            </button>
          </li>
          <li aria-hidden="true">/</li>
          <li aria-current="page" className="font-medium text-slate-300">
            {tool.name}
          </li>
        </ol>
      </nav>

      {/* Tool Header */}
      <div className="text-center space-y-3">
        <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-slate-700 bg-slate-800/80 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300">
          <Icon className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-indigo-400" />
          <span className="capitalize">{tool.category} Tool</span>
        </div>

        <h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-4xl font-extrabold tracking-tight text-white">
          {tool.metaH1}
        </h1>

        <p className="mx-auto max-w-2xl text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-300 leading-relaxed">
          {tool.detailedDescription}
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Main Interactive Tool Workplace Card */}
      <div className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/90 p-6 sm:p-8 shadow-2xl space-y-6">
        {/* Error message alert */}
        {errorMessage && (
          <div className="flex items-start gap-3 rounded-xl border border-rose-500/30 bg-rose-500/10 p-4 text-xs text-rose-300">
            <AlertCircle className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-rose-400 mt-0.5" />
            <div className="flex-1">
              <span className="font-semibold">Notice: </span>
              {errorMessage}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* If result is ready, display ResultCard */}
        {result ? (
          <ResultCard
            result={result}
            onReset={resetState}
          />
        ) : processingState !== 'idle' && processingState !== 'error' ? (
          /* Processing State Visualizer */
          <ProgressBar
            state={processingState}
            progressPercent={progressPercent}
            customMessage={statusMessage}
          />
        ) : (
          /* Tool Configuration & Upload State */
          <div className="space-y-6">
            {/* Special Utility Layout: Word Counter & Character Counter */}
            {['word-counter', 'character-counter'].includes(tool.id) ? (
              <div className="space-y-4">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-medium text-slate-400">
                  <span>Type or paste your text below:</span>
                  {utilityText && (
                    <button
                      onClick={() => setUtilityText('')}
                      className="text-rose-400 hover:text-rose-300"
                    >
                      Clear Text
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>

                <textarea
                  rows={8}
                  value={utilityText}
                  onChange={(e) => setUtilityText(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Paste or write your document text here..."
                  className="w-full rounded-2xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 p-4 text-sm text-white placeholder-slate-500 outline-none focus:border-indigo-500 font-sans"
                />

                {/* Live Statistics Cards */}
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-4">
                  <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-800/50 p-3 text-center">
                    <span className="text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-400">Words</span>
                    <p className="text-xl font-bold text-white mt-0.5">{textStats.words}</p>
                  </div>
                  <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-800/50 p-3 text-center">
                    <span className="text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-400">Characters</span>
                    <p className="text-xl font-bold text-indigo-400 mt-0.5">{textStats.characters}</p>
                  </div>
                  <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-800/50 p-3 text-center">
                    <span className="text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-400">Sentences</span>
                    <p className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-200 mt-0.5">{textStats.sentences}</p>
                  </div>
                  <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-800/50 p-3 text-center">
                    <span className="text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-400">Reading Time</span>
                    <p className="text-xs font-bold text-emerald-400 mt-1.5">{textStats.readingTime}</p>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Additional Metrics */}
                {tool.id === 'word-counter' && textStats.topKeywords.length > 0 && (
                  <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-800/30 p-4 space-y-2">
                    <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300">Top Keyword Density:</span>
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 pt-1">
                      {textStats.topKeywords.map((kw) => (
                        <span
                          key={kw.word}
                          className="rounded-lg bg-slate-800 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs text-slate-300 border border-slate-700/60"
                        >
                          <span className="font-semibold text-indigo-400">{kw.word}</span>: {kw.count} ({kw.percentage}%)
                        </span>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* Character Counter Case Converters */}
                {tool.id === 'character-counter' && (
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 pt-2">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setUtilityText((t) => t.toUpperCase())}
                      className="rounded-lg border border-slate-700 bg-slate-800 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-700"
                    >
                      UPPERCASE
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setUtilityText((t) => t.toLowerCase())}
                      className="rounded-lg border border-slate-700 bg-slate-800 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-700"
                    >
