                      lowercase
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() =>
                        setUtilityText((t) =>
                          t.replace(/\w\S*/g, (txt) => txt.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + txt.substr(1).toLowerCase())
                        )
                      }
                      className="rounded-lg border border-slate-700 bg-slate-800 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-700"
                    >
                      Title Case
                    </button>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            ) : (
              /* Standard File Upload Area */
              <FileUploader
                tool={tool}
                files={files}
                onFilesChanged={setFiles}
                onError={setErrorMessage}
              />
            )}

            {/* Tool-Specific Options Panel */}
            {files.length > 0 && (
              <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950/60 p-5 space-y-4">
                <h4 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400">
                  Tool Configuration
                </h4>

                {/* 1. Split PDF Options */}
                {tool.id === 'split-pdf' && (
                  <div className="space-y-3">
                    <div className="flex gap-4">
                      <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-slate-300 cursor-pointer">
                        <input
                          type="radio"
                          name="splitMode"
                          checked={splitMode === 'all_pages'}
                          onChange={() => setSplitMode('all_pages')}
                          className="accent-indigo-600"
                        />
                        Extract All Pages (ZIP bundle)
                      </label>
                      <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-slate-300 cursor-pointer">
                        <input
                          type="radio"
                          name="splitMode"
                          checked={splitMode === 'custom_ranges'}
                          onChange={() => setSplitMode('custom_ranges')}
                          className="accent-indigo-600"
                        />
                        Custom Page Ranges
                      </label>
                    </div>

                    {splitMode === 'custom_ranges' && (
                      <div className="space-y-1">
                        <label className="text-xs text-slate-400">
                          Enter page ranges (e.g. 1-3, 5):
                        </label>
                        <input
                          type="text"
                          value={splitRange}
                          onChange={(e) => setSplitRange(e.target.value)}
                          placeholder="e.g. 1-2, 4"
                          className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-3.5 py-2 text-xs text-white outline-none focus:border-indigo-500"
                        />
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* 2. Compress PDF Options */}
                {tool.id === 'compress-pdf' && (
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3">
                    {[
                      { id: 'high', label: 'High Compression', desc: 'Maximum size reduction, good quality' },
                      { id: 'recommended', label: 'Recommended', desc: 'Optimal balance of quality & size' },
                      { id: 'low', label: 'Low Compression', desc: 'Minimal compression, best quality' }
                    ].map((opt) => (
                      <div
                        key={opt.id}
                        onClick={() => setCompressLevel(opt.id as any)}
                        className={`cursor-pointer rounded-xl border p-3 transition-colors ${
                          compressLevel === opt.id
                            ? 'border-indigo-500 bg-indigo-500/10 text-white'
                            : 'border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/60 text-slate-300 hover:border-slate-700'
                        }`}
                      >
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                          <span className="text-xs font-bold">{opt.label}</span>
                          <span
                            className={`h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full ${
                              compressLevel === opt.id ? 'bg-indigo-400' : 'bg-slate-700'
                            }`}
                          />
                        </div>
                        <p className="mt-1 text-[11px] text-slate-400 leading-snug">{opt.desc}</p>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* 3. Image to PDF Layout Options */}
                {['jpg-to-pdf', 'png-to-pdf', 'image-to-pdf'].includes(tool.id) && (
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
                    <div>
                      <label className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-400">Page Size</label>
                      <select
                        value={imgPdfPageSize}
                        onChange={(e) => setImgPdfPageSize(e.target.value as any)}
                        className="mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white outline-none"
                      >
                        <option value="a4">A4 (Standard)</option>
                        <option value="letter">US Letter</option>
                        <option value="fit">Fit to Image</option>
                      </select>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-400">Orientation</label>
                      <select
                        value={imgPdfOrientation}
                        onChange={(e) => setImgPdfOrientation(e.target.value as any)}
                        className="mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white outline-none"
                      >
                        <option value="portrait">Portrait</option>
                        <option value="landscape">Landscape</option>
                      </select>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-400">Margins</label>
                      <select
                        value={imgPdfMargin}
                        onChange={(e) => setImgPdfMargin(e.target.value as any)}
                        className="mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white outline-none"
                      >
                        <option value="none">No Margin</option>
                        <option value="small">Small Margin</option>
                        <option value="large">Large Margin</option>
                      </select>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* 4. Compress Image Options */}
                {tool.id === 'compress-image' && (
                  <div className="space-y-4">
                    <div>
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-slate-300">
                        <span>Quality Level: {Math.round(imgQuality * 100)}%</span>
                        <span className="text-indigo-400">
                          {imgQuality < 0.4 ? 'Aggressive' : imgQuality < 0.8 ? 'Balanced' : 'High Quality'}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <input
                        type="range"
                        min="0.1"
                        max="0.95"
                        step="0.05"
                        value={imgQuality}
                        onChange={(e) => setImgQuality(parseFloat(e.target.value))}
                        className="mt-2 w-full accent-indigo-500"
                      />
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                      <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">Target Format:</span>
                      {(['image/jpeg', 'image/png', 'image/webp'] as const).map((fmt) => (
                        <button
                          key={fmt}
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => setImgFormat(fmt)}
                          className={`rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold uppercase ${
                            imgFormat === fmt ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white' : 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-400'
                          }`}
                        >
                          {fmt.split('/')[1]}
                        </button>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* 5. Resize Image Options */}
                {tool.id === 'resize-image' && (
                  <div className="space-y-3">
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
                      <div>
                        <label className="text-xs text-slate-400">Width (pixels)</label>
                        <input
                          type="number"
                          value={imgWidth}
                          onChange={(e) => handleWidthChange(parseInt(e.target.value, 10) || 0)}
                          className="mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white outline-none"
                        />
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <label className="text-xs text-slate-400">Height (pixels)</label>
                        <input
                          type="number"
                          value={imgHeight}
                          onChange={(e) => handleHeightChange(parseInt(e.target.value, 10) || 0)}
                          className="mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white outline-none"
                        />
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-slate-300 cursor-pointer">
                      <input
                        type="checkbox"
                        checked={lockAspect}
                        onChange={(e) => setLockAspect(e.target.checked)}
                        className="rounded accent-indigo-600"
                      />
                      Lock Aspect Ratio ({Math.round(aspectRatio * 100) / 100}:1)
                    </label>
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* 6. PDF Page Counter Metadata Inspection */}
                {tool.id === 'pdf-page-counter' && (
                  <div className="text-xs text-slate-300 space-y-1">
                    <p>File verified: <span className="font-semibold text-white">{files[0]?.name}</span></p>
                    <p>Ready to inspect document properties, page count, and dimensions.</p>
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* 7. File Size Checker Diagnostics */}
                {tool.id === 'file-size-checker' && files[0] && (
                  <div className="space-y-3">
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-2">
                      <div className="rounded-lg bg-slate-900 p-2.5">
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">Exact Bytes</span>
                        <p className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-white">{formatExactBytes(files[0].size)}</p>
                      </div>
                      <div className="rounded-lg bg-slate-900 p-2.5">
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">Kilobytes</span>
                        <p className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-white">{(files[0].size / 1024).toFixed(2)} KB</p>
                      </div>
                      <div className="rounded-lg bg-slate-900 p-2.5">
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">Megabytes</span>
                        <p className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-indigo-400">{(files[0].size / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(3)} MB</p>
                      </div>
                      <div className="rounded-lg bg-slate-900 p-2.5">
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">Gmail Limit</span>
                        <p className={`text-xs font-bold ${files[0].size <= 25 * 1024 * 1024 ? 'text-emerald-400' : 'text-rose-400'}`}>
                          {files[0].size <= 25 * 1024 * 1024 ? 'Within Limit (25MB)' : 'Exceeds Gmail Limit'}
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}

                                {/* 8. Page Number PDF Options */}
                {tool.id === 'page-number-pdf' && (
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                    <div>
                      <label className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-400">Position</label>
                      <select
                        value={pageNumPosition}
                        onChange={(e) => setPageNumPosition(e.target.value as any)}
                        className="mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white outline-none"
                      >
                        <option value="bottom-center">Bottom Center</option>
                        <option value="bottom-right">Bottom Right</option>
                        <option value="bottom-left">Bottom Left</option>
                        <option value="top-center">Top Center</option>
                        <option value="top-right">Top Right</option>
                      </select>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-400">Numbering Format</label>
                      <select
                        value={pageNumFormat}
                        onChange={(e) => setPageNumFormat(e.target.value as any)}
                        className="mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white outline-none"
                      >
                        <option value="page_x_of_y">Page 1 of N</option>
                        <option value="x_of_y">1 / N</option>
                        <option value="x">1 (Number only)</option>
                      </select>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-400">First Page Number</label>
                      <input
                        type="number"
                        min="1"
                        value={pageNumStart}
                        onChange={(e) => setPageNumStart(parseInt(e.target.value, 10) || 1)}
                        className="mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white outline-none"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* 9. Watermark PDF Options */}
                {tool.id === 'watermark-pdf' && (
                  <div className="space-y-4">
                    <div className="flex gap-4 border-b border-slate-800 pb-3">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setWmType('text')}
                        className={`text-xs font-semibold pb-1 border-b-2 transition-colors ${
                          wmType === 'text' ? 'border-indigo-500 text-indigo-400' : 'border-transparent text-slate-400 hover:text-white'
                        }`}
                      >
                        Text Stamp
                      </button>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setWmType('image')}
                        className={`text-xs font-semibold pb-1 border-b-2 transition-colors ${
                          wmType === 'image' ? 'border-indigo-500 text-indigo-400' : 'border-transparent text-slate-400 hover:text-white'
                        }`}
                      >
                        Logo / Image Watermark
                      </button>
                    </div>

                    {wmType === 'text' ? (
                      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                        <div>
                          <label className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-400">Watermark Text</label>
                          <input
                            type="text"
                            value={wmText}
                            onChange={(e) => setWmText(e.target.value)}
                            placeholder="e.g. CONFIDENTIAL, DRAFT"
                            className="mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white outline-none"
                          />
                        </div>

                        <div>
                          <label className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-400">Stamp Color</label>
                          <div className="mt-1 flex gap-2">
                            {(['gray', 'red', 'blue', 'black'] as const).map((c) => (
                              <button
                                key={c}
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => setWmColor(c)}
                                className={`rounded-lg px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold capitalize ${
                                  wmColor === c ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white' : 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-700'
                                }`}
                              >
                                {c}
                              </button>
                            ))}
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    ) : (
                      <div>
                        <label className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-400">Upload Watermark Image (PNG recommended)</label>
                        <input
                          type="file"
                          accept="image/png,image/jpeg"
                          onChange={(e) => {
                            if (e.target.files && e.target.files[0]) {
                              setWmImageFile(e.target.files[0]);
                            }
                          }}
                          className="mt-1 block w-full text-xs text-slate-400 file:mr-3 file:rounded-lg file:border-0 file:bg-indigo-600 file:px-3 file:py-1.5 file:text-xs file:font-semibold file:text-white hover:file:bg-indigo-500"
                        />
                        {wmImageFile && (
                          <p className="mt-1 text-[11px] text-emerald-400 font-medium">Loaded: {wmImageFile.name}</p>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    )}

                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4 pt-1">
                      <div>
                        <div className="flex justify-between text-xs text-slate-400 mb-1">
                          <span>Opacity: {Math.round(wmOpacity * 100)}%</span>
                        </div>
                        <input
                          type="range"
                          min="0.05"
                          max="0.8"
                          step="0.05"
                          value={wmOpacity}
                          onChange={(e) => setWmOpacity(parseFloat(e.target.value))}
                          className="w-full accent-indigo-500"
                        />
                      </div>

                      <div>
                        <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-400 block mb-1">Rotation Angle</span>
                        <div className="flex gap-2">
                          {[0, 45, 90].map((deg) => (
                            <button
                              key={deg}
                              type="button"
                              onClick={() => setWmRotation(deg)}
                              className={`rounded-lg px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold ${
                                wmRotation === deg ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white' : 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-300'
                              }`}
                            >
                              {deg}° {deg === 45 ? '(Diagonal)' : deg === 0 ? '(Horizontal)' : '(Vertical)'}
                            </button>
                          ))}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* 10. Edit PDF Metadata Options */}
                {tool.id === 'edit-pdf-metadata' && (
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                    <div>
                      <label className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-400">Document Title</label>
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        value={metaTitle}
                        onChange={(e) => setMetaTitle(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="e.g. Annual Financial Report"
                        className="mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white outline-none"
                      />
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-400">Author / Creator</label>
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        value={metaAuthor}
                        onChange={(e) => setMetaAuthor(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="e.g. John Doe"
                        className="mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white outline-none"
                      />
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-400">Subject</label>
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        value={metaSubject}
                        onChange={(e) => setMetaSubject(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="e.g. Financial Summary"
                        className="mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white outline-none"
                      />
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-400">Keywords (comma-separated)</label>
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        value={metaKeywords}
                        onChange={(e) => setMetaKeywords(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="e.g. finance, 2026, report"
                        className="mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white outline-none"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* 11. Flatten PDF Options */}
                {tool.id === 'flatten-pdf' && (
                  <div className="flex items-start gap-3 rounded-xl border border-indigo-500/20 bg-indigo-500/5 p-4 text-xs text-slate-300">
                    <FileCheck className="h-5 w-5 text-indigo-400 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                    <div className="space-y-1">
                      <p className="font-semibold text-white">Flatten Form Fields & Annotations</p>
                      <p className="text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
                        This tool merges all interactive form elements, checkboxes, radio selections, and comments directly into the base PDF vector layer, making them completely immutable and viewable across all devices.
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* 12. Sign PDF Canvas & Placement Options */}
                {tool.id === 'sign-pdf' && (
                  <div className="space-y-4">
                    <div>
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1.5">
                        <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300">Draw Your Signature:</span>
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => setSigPenColor('#1e3a8a')}
                            className={`h-4 w-4 rounded-full bg-blue-900 border ${sigPenColor === '#1e3a8a' ? 'ring-2 ring-indigo-400' : 'border-slate-600'}`}
                            title="Blue ink"
                          />
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => setSigPenColor('#000000')}
                            className={`h-4 w-4 rounded-full bg-black border ${sigPenColor === '#000000' ? 'ring-2 ring-indigo-400' : 'border-slate-600'}`}
                            title="Black ink"
                          />
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={clearSignatureCanvas}
                            className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] text-rose-400 hover:text-rose-300 ml-2"
                          >
                            <Eraser className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> Clear
                          </button>
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      <div className="rounded-xl border-2 border-dashed border-slate-700 bg-white p-1 overflow-hidden shadow-inner cursor-crosshair">
                        <canvas
                          ref={canvasRef}
                          width={440}
                          height={140}
                          className="w-full h-28 touch-none block"
                          onMouseDown={startDrawing}
                          onMouseMove={draw}
                          onMouseUp={stopDrawing}
                          onMouseLeave={stopDrawing}
                          onTouchStart={startDrawing}
                          onTouchMove={draw}
                          onTouchEnd={stopDrawing}
                        />
                      </div>
                      <p className="mt-1 text-[11px] text-slate-500">Sign with mouse, trackpad, or finger on mobile</p>
                    </div>

                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4 pt-1">
                      <div>
                        <label className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-400">Stamp Placement</label>
                        <select
                          value={sigPosition}
                          onChange={(e) => setSigPosition(e.target.value as any)}
                          className="mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white outline-none"
                        >
                          <option value="bottom-right">Bottom Right (Standard)</option>
                          <option value="bottom-left">Bottom Left</option>
                          <option value="bottom-center">Bottom Center</option>
                          <option value="center">Center of Page</option>
                        </select>
                      </div>

                      <div>
                        <label className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-400">Target Page</label>
                        <select
                          value={sigTargetPage}
                          onChange={(e) => setSigTargetPage(e.target.value as any)}
                          className="mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white outline-none"
                        >
                          <option value="last">Last Page (Recommended)</option>
                          <option value="first">First Page</option>
                          <option value="all">Every Page</option>
                        </select>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* 13. Resize PDF Pages Options */}
                {tool.id === 'resize-pdf-pages' && (
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                    <div>
                      <label className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-400">Target Paper Size</label>
                      <select
                        value={targetPdfPageSize}
                        onChange={(e) => setTargetPdfPageSize(e.target.value as any)}
                        className="mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white outline-none"
                      >
                        <option value="a4">A4 (Standard Global, 210 × 297 mm)</option>
                        <option value="letter">US Letter (8.5 × 11 in)</option>
                        <option value="legal">US Legal (8.5 × 14 in)</option>
                        <option value="a3">A3 (297 × 420 mm)</option>
                      </select>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-400">Page Orientation</label>
                      <select
                        value={targetPdfOrientation}
                        onChange={(e) => setTargetPdfOrientation(e.target.value as any)}
                        className="mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white outline-none"
                      >
                        <option value="portrait">Portrait</option>
                        <option value="landscape">Landscape</option>
                        <option value="match">Match Existing Aspect Ratio</option>
                      </select>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* 14. Crop PDF Pages Options */}
                {tool.id === 'crop-pdf' && (
                  <div className="space-y-3">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-slate-400">
                      <span>Trim Margins (points, 72 pt = 1 inch):</span>
                      <div className="flex gap-2">
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => { setCropTop(18); setCropBottom(18); setCropLeft(18); setCropRight(18); }}
                          className="rounded bg-slate-800 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-700"
                        >
                          Light (18pt)
                        </button>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => { setCropTop(36); setCropBottom(36); setCropLeft(36); setCropRight(36); }}
                          className="rounded bg-slate-800 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-700"
                        >
                          Medium (36pt)
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
                      <div>
                        <label className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">Top (pt)</label>
                        <input
                          type="number"
                          min="0"
                          max="200"
                          value={cropTop}
                          onChange={(e) => setCropTop(Math.max(0, parseInt(e.target.value, 10) || 0))}
                          className="mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs text-white outline-none"
                        />
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <label className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">Bottom (pt)</label>
                        <input
                          type="number"
                          min="0"
                          max="200"
                          value={cropBottom}
                          onChange={(e) => setCropBottom(Math.max(0, parseInt(e.target.value, 10) || 0))}
                          className="mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs text-white outline-none"
                        />
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <label className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">Left (pt)</label>
                        <input
                          type="number"
                          min="0"
                          max="200"
                          value={cropLeft}
                          onChange={(e) => setCropLeft(Math.max(0, parseInt(e.target.value, 10) || 0))}
                          className="mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs text-white outline-none"
                        />
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <label className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">Right (pt)</label>
                        <input
                          type="number"
                          min="0"
                          max="200"
                          value={cropRight}
                          onChange={(e) => setCropRight(Math.max(0, parseInt(e.target.value, 10) || 0))}
                          className="mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs text-white outline-none"
                        />
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* 15. Repair PDF Diagnostics */}
                {tool.id === 'repair-pdf' && (
                  <div className="flex items-start gap-3 rounded-xl border border-indigo-500/20 bg-indigo-500/5 p-4 text-xs text-slate-300">
                    <Wrench className="h-5 w-5 text-indigo-400 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                    <div className="space-y-1">
                      <p className="font-semibold text-white">Reconstruct Damaged PDF Stream</p>
                      <p className="text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
                        The repair engine will parse the document using lenient error-recovery mode, rebuild missing cross-reference tables (XREF), salvage readable pages, and output a validated PDF standard document.
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* 16. Unlock PDF Diagnostics */}
                {tool.id === 'unlock-pdf' && (
                  <div className="space-y-3">
                    <div className="flex items-start gap-3 rounded-xl border border-emerald-500/20 bg-emerald-500/5 p-4 text-xs text-slate-300">
                      <Unlock className="h-5 w-5 text-emerald-400 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                      <div className="space-y-1">
                        <p className="font-semibold text-white">Clear Document Restrictions</p>
                        <p className="text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
                          Removes owner restrictions on printing, copying, or editing. If a file merely restricts
                          permissions, the original is rebuilt unencrypted with text intact. If the PDF requires a
                          password just to <em>open</em> it, enter that password below — the result is rebuilt from
                          page images, so text will no longer be selectable.
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <label className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-400">
                        Password (only if this PDF asks for one to open)
                      </label>
                      <input
                        type="password"
                        value={unlockPassword}
                        onChange={(e) => setUnlockPassword(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="Leave blank if the PDF just opens restricted"
                        autoComplete="off"
                        className="mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white outline-none focus:border-indigo-500"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* Page Thumbnail View for Page Manipulation Tools */}
                {loadingPages && pdfPages.length === 0 && (
                  <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/40 py-12">
                    <div className="h-8 w-8 animate-spin rounded-full border-2 border-indigo-500 border-t-transparent" />
                    <p className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-400">Rendering page previews…</p>
                  </div>
                )}
                {pdfPages.length > 0 && (
                  <div className="pt-2">
                    <PageThumbnailGrid
                      pages={pdfPages}
                      mode={
                        tool.id === 'rotate-pdf'
                          ? 'rotate'
                          : tool.id === 'delete-pdf-pages'
                          ? 'delete'
                          : tool.id === 'extract-pdf-pages'
                          ? 'extract'
                          : 'reorder'
                      }
                      onRotatePage={handleRotateSinglePage}
                      onRotateAll={handleRotateAll}
                      onToggleSelect={handleToggleSelectPage}
                      onSelectAll={handleSelectAllPages}
                      onDeselectAll={handleDeselectAllPages}
                      onMovePage={handleMovePage}
                    />
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Main Action Trigger Button */}
            {(!['word-counter', 'character-counter'].includes(tool.id) || utilityText) && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleExecuteProcessing}
                disabled={files.length === 0 && !utilityText}
                className="flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 px-6 py-4 text-sm font-bold text-white shadow-xl shadow-indigo-600/30 transition-all hover:bg-indigo-500 hover:scale-[1.01] active:scale-[0.99] disabled:opacity-40 disabled:hover:scale-100 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
              >
                <Icon className="h-5 w-5" />
                Process & {tool.name}
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* SEO Content: How It Works */}
      <section className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/60 p-6 sm:p-8 space-y-6">
        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold tracking-tight text-white">
          How to Use {tool.name}
        </h2>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 md:grid-cols-3">
          {tool.steps.map((st) => (
            <div key={st.step} className="flex items-start gap-3">
              <span className="flex h-7 w-7 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-indigo-600 text-xs font-bold text-white">
                {st.step}
              </span>
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold text-white">{st.title}</h3>
                <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">{st.description}</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Features & Benefits */}
      <section className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/40 p-6 sm:p-8 space-y-6">
        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold tracking-tight text-white">Key Features</h2>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
          {tool.features.map((feat, idx) => (
            <div key={idx} className="rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/80 p-4">
              <h3 className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold text-indigo-400">{feat.title}</h3>
              <p className="mt-1.5 text-xs text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">{feat.description}</p>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* FAQ Section */}
      <FAQAccordion faqs={tool.faqs} />

      {/* Related Tools */}
      <RelatedTools
        currentSlug={tool.slug}
        relatedSlugs={tool.relatedToolSlugs}
        onNavigate={(path) => onNavigate(path.startsWith('/') ? path : `/${path}`)}
      />
    </div>
  );
};

======================================================================
FILE: src/services/analyticsService.ts
======================================================================
import { ToolUsageStat } from '../types';

export interface JobRecord {
  id: string;
  toolSlug: string;
  toolName: string;
  timestamp: string;
  originalSize: number;
  processedSize: number;
  durationMs: number;
  status: 'completed' | 'failed';
  errorMessage?: string;
}

const STATS_KEY = 'docuforge_analytics_stats_v1';
const JOBS_KEY = 'docuforge_analytics_jobs_v1';

export class AnalyticsService {
  private static getStoredStats(): Record<string, ToolUsageStat> {
    try {
      const data = localStorage.getItem(STATS_KEY);
      return data ? JSON.parse(data) : {};
    } catch {
      return {};
    }
  }

  private static saveStats(stats: Record<string, ToolUsageStat>): void {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(STATS_KEY, JSON.stringify(stats));
    } catch {
      // storage quota or private mode fallback
    }
  }

  public static getRecentJobs(): JobRecord[] {
    try {
      const data = localStorage.getItem(JOBS_KEY);
      if (data) return JSON.parse(data);
    } catch {
      // fallback
    }
    // No jobs recorded yet in this browser — return an honest empty list.
    // The UI shows a proper empty state rather than fabricated audit rows.
    return [];
  }

  public static trackJob(record: Omit<JobRecord, 'id' | 'timestamp'>): void {
    const jobs = this.getRecentJobs();
    const newJob: JobRecord = {
      ...record,
      id: 'job-' + Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000),
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
    };
    jobs.unshift(newJob);
    if (jobs.length > 50) jobs.pop();

    try {
      localStorage.setItem(JOBS_KEY, JSON.stringify(jobs));
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }

    // Update aggregated stats
    const stats = this.getStoredStats();
    const current = stats[record.toolSlug] || {
      toolId: record.toolSlug,
      toolName: record.toolName,
      count: 0,
      bytesProcessed: 0,
      successCount: 0,
      failCount: 0,
      lastUsed: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    current.count += 1;
    current.bytesProcessed += record.originalSize;
    if (record.status === 'completed') {
      current.successCount += 1;
    } else {
      current.failCount += 1;
    }
    current.lastUsed = new Date().toISOString();
    stats[record.toolSlug] = current;
    this.saveStats(stats);
  }

  public static getAggregatedStats(): {
    totalJobs: number;
    successRate: number;
    totalBytes: number;
    toolsBreakdown: ToolUsageStat[];
  } {
    const stats = this.getStoredStats();
    const list = Object.values(stats);

    let totalJobs = 0;
    let totalSuccess = 0;
    let totalBytes = 0;

    list.forEach((item) => {
      totalJobs += item.count;
      totalSuccess += item.successCount;
      totalBytes += item.bytesProcessed;
    });

    // Real figures only. No seeded/phantom activity — an unused install
    // honestly shows zero, and a 100% rate is meaningless with 0 jobs.
    const successRate = totalJobs > 0 ? (totalSuccess / totalJobs) * 100 : 0;

    return {
      totalJobs,
      successRate: Math.round(successRate * 10) / 10,
      totalBytes,
      toolsBreakdown: list.sort((a, b) => b.count - a.count)
    };
  }
}

======================================================================
FILE: src/services/conversionEngine.ts
======================================================================
import { PDFDocument, rgb, StandardFonts } from 'pdf-lib';
import JSZip from 'jszip';
import { RenderEngine } from './renderEngine';
import { createWinAnsiSanitizer, wrapLine } from '../utils/pdfText';

export class ConversionEngine {
  /**
   * PDF to Word (.docx):
   * Extracts text runs and lines from the PDF and creates an authentic,
   * fully-editable Microsoft Word .docx file using the docx package.
   * DOCX text runs are plain Unicode, so nothing here is limited to WinAnsi.
   */
  public static async pdfToWord(file: File): Promise<{ blob: Blob; fileName: string; textPreview: string }> {
    const { Document: DocxDocument, Paragraph, TextRun, HeadingLevel, Packer } = await import('docx');

    const pdf = await RenderEngine.openPDF(await file.arrayBuffer());
    const docxParagraphs: any[] = [];
    let extractedSample = '';

    try {
      const totalPages = pdf.numPages;

      for (let pageNum = 1; pageNum <= totalPages; pageNum++) {
        const page = await pdf.getPage(pageNum);
        let items: Array<{ str: string; transform: number[] }>;
        try {
          const textContent = await page.getTextContent();
          items = textContent.items as any;
        } finally {
          page.cleanup();
        }

        // Group items by vertical Y coordinate to reconstruct lines
        const lineMap = new Map<number, string[]>();
        items.forEach((item) => {
          if (!item.str || item.str.trim() === '') return;
          const y = Math.round((item.transform ? item.transform[5] : 0) / 4) * 4;
          const currentLine = lineMap.get(y) || [];
          currentLine.push(item.str);
          lineMap.set(y, currentLine);
        });

        // Sort lines by Y descending (PDF coordinates origin is bottom-left)
        const sortedY = Array.from(lineMap.keys()).sort((a, b) => b - a);

        if (pageNum > 1) {
          docxParagraphs.push(
            new Paragraph({
              text: `--- Page ${pageNum} ---`,
              heading: HeadingLevel.HEADING_3,
              spacing: { before: 400, after: 200 }
            })
          );
        }

        for (const y of sortedY) {
          const lineText = lineMap.get(y)?.join(' ') || '';
          if (!lineText.trim()) continue;

          if (extractedSample.length < 500) extractedSample += lineText + '\n';

          const isHeading = lineText.length < 60 && (lineText === lineText.toUpperCase() || lineText.endsWith(':'));

          docxParagraphs.push(
            new Paragraph({
              children: [
                new TextRun({
                  text: lineText,
                  bold: isHeading,
                  size: isHeading ? 28 : 22,
                  color: isHeading ? '1E293B' : '334155',
                  font: 'Calibri'
                })
              ],
              spacing: { after: 120 }
            })
          );
        }
      }
    } finally {
      await pdf.destroy();
    }

    if (docxParagraphs.length === 0) {
      docxParagraphs.push(
        new Paragraph({
          children: [
            new TextRun({
              text: 'Note: Document contained no selectable text streams. If this was a scanned image, please use the Scanned PDF OCR tool.',
              italics: true,
              color: 'DC2626'
            })
          ]
        })
      );
    }

    const doc = new DocxDocument({ sections: [{ properties: {}, children: docxParagraphs }] });
    const docxBlob = await Packer.toBlob(doc);
    const baseName = file.name.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, '');

    return {
      blob: docxBlob,
      fileName: `${baseName}.docx`,
      textPreview: extractedSample || 'Text extracted successfully into DOCX document.'
    };
  }

  /**
   * Word / Text to PDF:
   * Generates a clean, paginated, print-ready PDF from text/document content.
   * If a Word (.docx) file is provided, it extracts paragraphs from word/document.xml.
   */
  public static async textToPDF(file: File): Promise<Blob> {
    let text = '';
    const isDocx = file.name.toLowerCase().endsWith('.docx') || file.type.includes('wordprocessingml');

    if (isDocx) {
      try {
        const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(await file.arrayBuffer());
        const docXmlFile = zip.file('word/document.xml');
        if (docXmlFile) {
          const xmlText = await docXmlFile.async('text');
          const parser = new DOMParser();
          const xmlDoc = parser.parseFromString(xmlText, 'application/xml');
          if (xmlDoc.getElementsByTagName('parsererror').length > 0) {
            throw new Error('Malformed document.xml');
          }
          const paragraphs = xmlDoc.getElementsByTagName('w:p');
          const lines: string[] = [];
          for (let i = 0; i < paragraphs.length; i++) {
            const texts = paragraphs[i].getElementsByTagName('w:t');
            let pStr = '';
            for (let j = 0; j < texts.length; j++) pStr += texts[j].textContent || '';
            lines.push(pStr);
          }
          text = lines.join('\n');
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn('DOCX XML parsing error, falling back to plain text:', e);
      }
    }

    if (!text) {
      try {
        text = await file.text();
      } catch {
        text = `Document: ${file.name}\n(Content could not be decoded as plain text)`;
      }
    }
    if (!text.trim()) {
      text = `Document: ${file.name}\n(No readable text content was found in this file.)`;
    }

    const pdfDoc = await PDFDocument.create();
    const font = await pdfDoc.embedFont(StandardFonts.Helvetica);
    const fontBold = await pdfDoc.embedFont(StandardFonts.HelveticaBold);
    const sanitizer = createWinAnsiSanitizer(font);

    const pageWidth = 595.28; // A4
    const pageHeight = 841.89;
    const margin = 50;
    const contentWidth = pageWidth - margin * 2;
    const lineHeight = 18;
    const bodySize = 10.5;

    let currentPage = pdfDoc.addPage([pageWidth, pageHeight]);
    let currentY = pageHeight - margin;

    const titleText = sanitizer.sanitize(file.name.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, '').toUpperCase());
    currentPage.drawText(titleText, { x: margin, y: currentY, size: 16, font: fontBold, color: rgb(0.12, 0.23, 0.44) });
    currentY -= 28;
    currentPage.drawLine({
      start: { x: margin, y: currentY },
      end: { x: pageWidth - margin, y: currentY },
      thickness: 1,
      color: rgb(0.8, 0.85, 0.9)
    });
    currentY -= 20;

    const newPage = () => {
      currentPage = pdfDoc.addPage([pageWidth, pageHeight]);
      currentY = pageHeight - margin;
    };

    for (const rawLine of text.split(/\r?\n/)) {
      const clean = sanitizer.sanitize(rawLine);
      const wrapped = wrapLine(clean, font, bodySize, contentWidth);

      for (const line of wrapped) {
        if (currentY <= margin + lineHeight) newPage();
        if (line) {
          currentPage.drawText(line, { x: margin, y: currentY, size: bodySize, font, color: rgb(0.15, 0.15, 0.2) });
        }
        currentY -= lineHeight;
      }
      if (clean === '') currentY -= 8;
    }

    const pdfBytes = await pdfDoc.save({ useObjectStreams: true });
    return new Blob([pdfBytes as any], { type: 'application/pdf' });
  }

  /**
   * PDF to Excel / CSV:
   * Detects tabular structure and columnar text from a PDF and exports it as CSV data.
   */
  public static async pdfToExcel(file: File): Promise<{ blob: Blob; fileName: string; rowCount: number }> {
    const pdf = await RenderEngine.openPDF(await file.arrayBuffer());
    const csvRows: string[] = [];

    try {
      for (let pageNum = 1; pageNum <= pdf.numPages; pageNum++) {
        const page = await pdf.getPage(pageNum);
        let items: Array<{ str: string; transform: number[] }>;
        try {
          const textContent = await page.getTextContent();
          items = textContent.items as any;
        } finally {
          page.cleanup();
        }

        const rowMap = new Map<number, Array<{ x: number; text: string }>>();
        items.forEach((item) => {
          if (!item.str || item.str.trim() === '') return;
          const y = Math.round((item.transform ? item.transform[5] : 0) / 6) * 6;
          const x = Math.round(item.transform ? item.transform[4] : 0);
          const row = rowMap.get(y) || [];
          row.push({ x, text: item.str });
          rowMap.set(y, row);
        });

        const sortedY = Array.from(rowMap.keys()).sort((a, b) => b - a);
        for (const y of sortedY) {
          const cells = rowMap.get(y)?.sort((a, b) => a.x - b.x) || [];
          const escaped = cells.map((c) => `"${c.text.replace(/"/g, '""').trim()}"`);
          if (escaped.length > 0) csvRows.push(escaped.join(','));
        }
      }
    } finally {
      await pdf.destroy();
    }

    // Excel/Sheets need a UTF-8 BOM to display accented/non-Latin text correctly.
    const csvContent = '\uFEFF' + csvRows.join('\r\n');
    const blob = new Blob([csvContent], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' });
    const baseName = file.name.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, '');

    return { blob, fileName: `${baseName}-data.csv`, rowCount: csvRows.length };
  }

  /**
   * Excel / CSV to PDF:
   * Formats CSV and Excel (.xlsx) tables into a clean, paginated PDF report.
   */
  public static async excelToPDF(file: File): Promise<Blob> {
    let rows: string[][] = [];
    const isXlsx = file.name.toLowerCase().endsWith('.xlsx') || file.type.includes('spreadsheetml');

    if (isXlsx) {
      try {
        const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(await file.arrayBuffer());
        const sharedStrings: string[] = [];
        const ssFile = zip.file('xl/sharedStrings.xml');
        if (ssFile) {
          const ssDoc = new DOMParser().parseFromString(await ssFile.async('text'), 'application/xml');
          const siNodes = ssDoc.getElementsByTagName('si');
          for (let i = 0; i < siNodes.length; i++) {
            const tNodes = siNodes[i].getElementsByTagName('t');
            let str = '';
            for (let j = 0; j < tNodes.length; j++) str += tNodes[j].textContent || '';
            sharedStrings.push(str);
          }
        }

        // Read every worksheet, not just the first, so a report never silently drops sheets.
        const sheetFiles = Object.keys(zip.files)
          .filter((name) => /^xl\/worksheets\/sheet\d+\.xml$/.test(name))
          .sort((a, b) => {
            const na = parseInt(a.match(/(\d+)/)?.[1] || '0', 10);
            const nb = parseInt(b.match(/(\d+)/)?.[1] || '0', 10);
            return na - nb;
          });

        for (const sheetPath of sheetFiles) {
          const sheetDoc = new DOMParser().parseFromString(await zip.file(sheetPath)!.async('text'), 'application/xml');
          const rowNodes = sheetDoc.getElementsByTagName('row');
          for (let i = 0; i < rowNodes.length; i++) {
            const cellNodes = rowNodes[i].getElementsByTagName('c');
            const rowCells: string[] = [];
            for (let j = 0; j < cellNodes.length; j++) {
              const c = cellNodes[j];
              const tType = c.getAttribute('t');
              const vNode = c.getElementsByTagName('v')[0];
              let val = vNode ? vNode.textContent || '' : '';
              if (tType === 's') val = sharedStrings[parseInt(val, 10)] || val;
              rowCells.push(val);
            }
            if (rowCells.some((c) => c.trim())) rows.push(rowCells);
          }
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn('XLSX parsing failed, falling back to CSV parser:', err);
      }
    }

    if (rows.length === 0) {
      const text = await file.text();
      rows = text
        .split(/\r?\n/)
        .map((r) => r.trim())
        .filter(Boolean)
        .map((rowStr) => {
          const pattern = /(?:^|,)(?:"([^"]*(?:""[^"]*)*)"|([^,]*))/g;
          const entries: string[] = [];
          let match;
          let lastIndex = 0;
          while ((match = pattern.exec(rowStr)) && match.index >= lastIndex) {
            if (match[0] === '' && entries.length > 0) break;
            entries.push((match[1] !== undefined ? match[1].replace(/""/g, '"') : match[2] || '').trim());
            lastIndex = pattern.lastIndex;
          }
          return entries;
        });
    }

    if (rows.length === 0) {
      throw new Error('No rows could be read from this file. Check that it is a valid spreadsheet or CSV.');
    }

    const pdfDoc = await PDFDocument.create();
    const font = await pdfDoc.embedFont(StandardFonts.Helvetica);
    const fontBold = await pdfDoc.embedFont(StandardFonts.HelveticaBold);
    const sanitizer = createWinAnsiSanitizer(font);

    const pageWidth = 841.89; // Landscape A4 for wide tables
    const pageHeight = 595.28;
    const margin = 40;
    const rowHeight = 22;

    const maxCols = Math.min(Math.max(...rows.map((r) => r.length), 1), 8);
    const colWidth = (pageWidth - margin * 2) / maxCols;
    const maxCharsPerCell = Math.max(6, Math.floor(colWidth / 6) - 2);

    let currentPage = pdfDoc.addPage([pageWidth, pageHeight]);
    let currentY = pageHeight - margin;

    currentPage.drawText(sanitizer.sanitize(`Spreadsheet Report: ${file.name}`), {
      x: margin,
      y: currentY,
      size: 14,
      font: fontBold,
      color: rgb(0.1, 0.2, 0.4)
    });
    currentY -= 26;

    rows.forEach((row, rowIndex) => {
      if (currentY <= margin + rowHeight) {
        currentPage = pdfDoc.addPage([pageWidth, pageHeight]);
        currentY = pageHeight - margin;
      }

      const isHeader = rowIndex === 0;
      if (isHeader) {
        currentPage.drawRectangle({ x: margin, y: currentY - 5, width: pageWidth - margin * 2, height: rowHeight, color: rgb(0.9, 0.93, 0.98) });
      } else if (rowIndex % 2 === 1) {
        currentPage.drawRectangle({ x: margin, y: currentY - 5, width: pageWidth - margin * 2, height: rowHeight, color: rgb(0.98, 0.98, 0.99) });
      }

      row.slice(0, maxCols).forEach((cellText, colIndex) => {
        const x = margin + colIndex * colWidth;
        const clean = sanitizer.sanitize(cellText);
        const truncated = clean.length > maxCharsPerCell ? clean.substring(0, maxCharsPerCell - 1) + '…' : clean;
        currentPage.drawText(truncated, {
          x: x + 6,
          y: currentY,
          size: 9.5,
          font: isHeader ? fontBold : font,
          color: isHeader ? rgb(0.1, 0.2, 0.4) : rgb(0.2, 0.2, 0.2)
        });
      });

      currentPage.drawLine({
        start: { x: margin, y: currentY - 5 },
        end: { x: pageWidth - margin, y: currentY - 5 },
        thickness: 0.5,
        color: rgb(0.85, 0.88, 0.92)
      });

      currentY -= rowHeight;
    });

    const pdfBytes = await pdfDoc.save({ useObjectStreams: true });
    return new Blob([pdfBytes as any], { type: 'application/pdf' });
  }

  /**
   * PowerPoint / Presentation to PDF:
   * Generates a modern 16:9 widescreen presentation slide deck PDF from a text/Markdown outline.
   */
  public static async presentationToPDF(file: File): Promise<Blob> {
    const text = await file.text();
    if (!text.trim()) {
      throw new Error('This file has no text content to turn into slides.');
    }
    const rawSlides = text.split(/(?:^|\n)(?:---|===|Slide\s*\d+:?)/gi).filter((s) => s.trim().length > 0);
    const slides = rawSlides.length > 0 ? rawSlides : [text];

    const pdfDoc = await PDFDocument.create();
    const font = await pdfDoc.embedFont(StandardFonts.Helvetica);
    const fontBold = await pdfDoc.embedFont(StandardFonts.HelveticaBold);
    const sanitizer = createWinAnsiSanitizer(font);

    const slideWidth = 960;
    const slideHeight = 540;
    const contentWidth = slideWidth - 95 - 60;

    slides.forEach((slideContent, index) => {
      const page = pdfDoc.addPage([slideWidth, slideHeight]);
      page.drawRectangle({ x: 0, y: 0, width: slideWidth, height: slideHeight, color: rgb(0.96, 0.97, 0.99) });
      page.drawRectangle({ x: 0, y: 0, width: 16, height: slideHeight, color: rgb(0.3, 0.45, 0.95) });

      const lines = slideContent.split(/\r?\n/).map((l) => sanitizer.sanitize(l.trim())).filter(Boolean);
      const title = lines[0] || `Slide ${index + 1}`;
      const bullets = lines.slice(1, 9); // cap bullets so they can't run off the bottom of the slide

      page.drawText(title, { x: 60, y: slideHeight - 80, size: 26, font: fontBold, color: rgb(0.08, 0.12, 0.25) });
      page.drawLine({
        start: { x: 60, y: slideHeight - 100 },
        end: { x: slideWidth - 60, y: slideHeight - 100 },
        thickness: 2,
        color: rgb(0.8, 0.85, 0.92)
      });

      let yPos = slideHeight - 145;
      for (const bullet of bullets) {
        if (yPos < 50) break;
        const bulletText = bullet.startsWith('-') || bullet.startsWith('*') ? bullet.substring(1).trim() : bullet;
        const wrapped = wrapLine(bulletText, font, 15, contentWidth).slice(0, 2); // cap wrap to 2 lines/bullet
        page.drawText('•', { x: 70, y: yPos, size: 16, font: fontBold, color: rgb(0.3, 0.45, 0.95) });
        for (const line of wrapped) {
          if (yPos < 40) break;
          page.drawText(line, { x: 95, y: yPos, size: 15, font, color: rgb(0.2, 0.25, 0.35) });
          yPos -= 22;
        }
        yPos -= 14;
      }

      page.drawText(`Slide ${index + 1} of ${slides.length}`, { x: slideWidth - 140, y: 30, size: 10, font, color: rgb(0.5, 0.55, 0.65) });
    });

    const bytes = await pdfDoc.save({ useObjectStreams: true });
    return new Blob([bytes as any], { type: 'application/pdf' });
  }

  /**
   * PDF to PowerPoint:
   * Extracts slides as high-res presentation graphics and an outline in a ZIP package.
   */
  public static async pdfToPowerPoint(file: File): Promise<{ blob: Blob; fileName: string; slideCount: number }> {
    const pdf = await RenderEngine.openPDF(await file.arrayBuffer());
    try {
      const slideCount = pdf.numPages;
      const zip = new JSZip();
      let outlineContent = `Presentation Outline & Speaker Notes\nSource: ${file.name}\nTotal Slides: ${slideCount}\n\n`;
      const padWidth = Math.max(2, String(slideCount).length);

      for (let p = 1; p <= slideCount; p++) {
        const page = await pdf.getPage(p);
        let pageText: string;
        try {
          const textContent = await page.getTextContent();
          pageText = textContent.items.map((i: any) => i.str).join(' ').trim();
        } finally {
          page.cleanup();
        }
        outlineContent += `--- Slide ${p} ---\n${pageText}\n\n`;

        const rendered = await RenderEngine.renderPage(pdf, p, 1.5, 'image/png', 1, false);
        zip.file(`slide-${p.toString().padStart(padWidth, '0')}.png`, rendered.blob);
      }

      zip.file('presentation-outline.txt', outlineContent);
      const zipBlob = await zip.generateAsync({ type: 'blob' });
      const baseName = file.name.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, '');

      return { blob: zipBlob, fileName: `${baseName}-slides.zip`, slideCount };
    } finally {
      await pdf.destroy();
    }
  }
}

======================================================================
FILE: src/services/imageEngine.ts

export interface ImageProcessResult {
  blob: Blob;
  dataUrl: string;
  originalSize: number;
  newSize: number;
  reductionPercentage: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  format: string;
}

export class ImageEngine {
  /**
   * Compress image with quality slider and format option
   */
  public static async compressImage(
    file: File,
    quality: number = 0.75, // 0.1 to 1.0
    outputFormat: 'image/jpeg' | 'image/png' | 'image/webp' = 'image/jpeg'
  ): Promise<ImageProcessResult> {
    const originalSize = file.size;
    const img = await this.loadImage(file);

    const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    canvas.width = img.naturalWidth;
    canvas.height = img.naturalHeight;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    if (!ctx) throw new Error('Canvas context initialization failed.');

    // Solid white background for JPEG
    if (outputFormat === 'image/jpeg') {
      ctx.fillStyle = '#FFFFFF';
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
    }
    ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0);

    const blob = await new Promise<Blob>((resolve, reject) => {
      canvas.toBlob(
        (b) => {
          if (b) resolve(b);
          else reject(new Error('Image encoding failed.'));
        },
        outputFormat,
        quality
      );
    });

    const newSize = blob.size;
    const reductionPercentage = Math.max(0, Math.round(((originalSize - newSize) / originalSize) * 100));
    const dataUrl = canvas.toDataURL(outputFormat, quality);

    return {
      blob,
      dataUrl,
      originalSize,
      newSize,
      reductionPercentage,
      width: img.naturalWidth,
      height: img.naturalHeight,
      format: outputFormat.split('/')[1]
    };
  }

  /**
   * Resize image to custom dimensions with optional aspect ratio preservation
   */
  public static async resizeImage(
    file: File,
    targetWidth: number,
    targetHeight: number,
    outputFormat: 'image/jpeg' | 'image/png' | 'image/webp' = 'image/jpeg',
    quality: number = 0.9
  ): Promise<ImageProcessResult> {
    const originalSize = file.size;
    const img = await this.loadImage(file);

    const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    canvas.width = Math.max(1, Math.round(targetWidth));
    canvas.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(targetHeight));
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    if (!ctx) throw new Error('Canvas context initialization failed.');

    if (outputFormat === 'image/jpeg') {
      ctx.fillStyle = '#FFFFFF';
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
    }

    // High quality bicubic image smoothing
    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = true;
    ctx.imageSmoothingQuality = 'high';
    ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

    const blob = await new Promise<Blob>((resolve, reject) => {
      canvas.toBlob(
        (b) => {
          if (b) resolve(b);
          else reject(new Error('Image resizing encoding failed.'));
        },
        outputFormat,
        quality
      );
    });

    const newSize = blob.size;
    const reductionPercentage = Math.round(((originalSize - newSize) / originalSize) * 100);
    const dataUrl = canvas.toDataURL(outputFormat, quality);

    return {
      blob,
      dataUrl,
      originalSize,
      newSize,
      reductionPercentage,
      width: canvas.width,
      height: canvas.height,
      format: outputFormat.split('/')[1]
    };
  }

  /**
   * Format converter between JPG, PNG, and WebP
   */
  public static async convertFormat(
    file: File,
    targetMime: 'image/jpeg' | 'image/png' | 'image/webp',
    quality: number = 0.92
  ): Promise<ImageProcessResult> {
    return this.compressImage(file, quality, targetMime);
  }

  /**
   * Helper to load an image file into an HTMLImageElement
   */
  public static loadImage(file: File): Promise<HTMLImageElement> {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const img = new Image();
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(file);
      img.onload = () => {
        URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
        resolve(img);
      };
      img.onerror = () => {
        URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
        reject(new Error(`Failed to load image file "${file.name}". Ensure the file is not corrupted.`));
      };
      img.src = url;
    });
  }
}

import { RenderEngine, PDFPasswordError } from './renderEngine';

export interface OCRResult {
  text: string;
  confidence: number;
  txtBlob: Blob;
  docxBlob: Blob;
  previewSnippet: string;
}

export class OCREngine {
  /**
   * Run OCR on an image file (JPG, PNG, WebP)
   */
  public static async recognizeImage(
    file: File,
    onProgress?: (progress: number, status: string) => void
  ): Promise<OCRResult> {
    onProgress?.(0.1, 'Initializing OCR engine...');

    const { createWorker } = await import('tesseract.js');

    const worker = await createWorker('eng', 1, {
      logger: (m: any) => {
        if (m.status === 'recognizing text' && m.progress !== undefined) {
          onProgress?.(0.2 + m.progress * 0.7, `Recognizing text (${Math.round(m.progress * 100)}%)...`);
        } else if (m.status) {
          onProgress?.(0.15, m.status);
        }
      }
    });

    try {
      onProgress?.(0.3, 'Analyzing image character glyphs...');
      const result = await worker.recognize(file);
      const text = result.data.text.trim();
      const confidence = Math.round(result.data.confidence);

      onProgress?.(0.95, 'Formatting text output...');

      const txtBlob = new Blob([text], { type: 'text/plain;charset=utf-8;' });
      const docxBlob = await this.buildDocxFromText(text, file.name);

      return {
        text,
        confidence,
        txtBlob,
        docxBlob,
        previewSnippet: text.slice(0, 300) || 'No text detected in image.'
      };
    } finally {
      await worker.terminate();
    }
  }

  /**
   * Extract selectable text from a digital PDF's text layer.
   */
  public static async extractPDFText(
    file: File,
    onProgress?: (current: number, total: number) => void
  ): Promise<{ text: string; pageCount: number; txtBlob: Blob; docxBlob: Blob }> {
    const pdf = await RenderEngine.openPDF(await file.arrayBuffer());
    try {
      const totalPages = pdf.numPages;
      let fullText = '';
      let sawAnyText = false;

      for (let p = 1; p <= totalPages; p++) {
        const page = await pdf.getPage(p);
        try {
          const textContent = await page.getTextContent();
          const pageStrings = textContent.items.map((i: any) => i.str).filter(Boolean);
          const pageText = pageStrings.join(' ').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
          if (pageText) sawAnyText = true;
          fullText += `--- Page ${p} ---\n${pageText}\n\n`;
        } finally {
          page.cleanup();
        }
        onProgress?.(p, totalPages);
      }

      if (!sawAnyText) {
        fullText =
          'No selectable text was found in this PDF. If it is a scan or photo of a document, use the "Scanned PDF OCR" tool instead.';
      }

      const cleanText = fullText.trim();
      const txtBlob = new Blob([cleanText], { type: 'text/plain;charset=utf-8;' });
      const docxBlob = await this.buildDocxFromText(cleanText, file.name);

      return { text: cleanText, pageCount: totalPages, txtBlob, docxBlob };
    } finally {
      await pdf.destroy();
    }
  }

  /**
   * Scanned PDF OCR: renders each page to an image and runs OCR across the raster bitmaps.
   * The PDF is opened once and reused for every page (previously each page reopened and
   * re-parsed the entire document, which was both slow and, once a shared render helper
   * started copying/consuming the buffer per call, unreliable for pages after the first).
   */
  public static async recognizeScannedPDF(
    file: File,
    onProgress?: (progress: number, status: string) => void
  ): Promise<OCRResult> {
    onProgress?.(0.05, 'Reading PDF structure...');
    const pdf = await RenderEngine.openPDF(await file.arrayBuffer());
    const MAX_PAGES = 10; // keep in-browser OCR responsive
    const totalPages = Math.min(pdf.numPages, MAX_PAGES);
    const truncated = pdf.numPages > MAX_PAGES;

    const { createWorker } = await import('tesseract.js');
    const worker = await createWorker('eng', 1);

    try {
      let aggregatedText = '';
      let totalConfidence = 0;

      for (let p = 1; p <= totalPages; p++) {
        onProgress?.(0.1 + (p / totalPages) * 0.8, `Running OCR on page ${p} of ${totalPages}...`);

        const pageImg = await RenderEngine.renderPage(pdf, p, 1.8, 'image/jpeg', 0.9, false);
        const res = await worker.recognize(pageImg.blob);

        aggregatedText += `--- Page ${p} ---\n${res.data.text.trim()}\n\n`;
        totalConfidence += res.data.confidence;
      }

      if (truncated) {
        aggregatedText += `\n[Only the first ${MAX_PAGES} of ${pdf.numPages} pages were processed to keep in-browser OCR responsive.]\n`;
      }

      onProgress?.(0.95, 'Compiling document...');

      const averageConfidence = totalPages > 0 ? Math.round(totalConfidence / totalPages) : 0;
      const txtBlob = new Blob([aggregatedText], { type: 'text/plain;charset=utf-8;' });
      const docxBlob = await this.buildDocxFromText(aggregatedText, file.name);

      return {
        text: aggregatedText,
        confidence: averageConfidence,
        txtBlob,
        docxBlob,
        previewSnippet: aggregatedText.slice(0, 300)
      };
    } finally {
      await worker.terminate();
      await pdf.destroy();
    }
  }

  /**
   * Helper to format text lines into an authentic DOCX file. DOCX text runs are plain
   * Unicode strings (unlike pdf-lib's built-in PDF fonts), so no font-encoding limits apply
   * here — any script the source document contained comes through unchanged.
   */
  private static async buildDocxFromText(text: string, sourceName: string): Promise<Blob> {
    const { Document, Paragraph, TextRun, Packer, HeadingLevel } = await import('docx');

    const titleParagraph = new Paragraph({
      heading: HeadingLevel.HEADING_1,
      spacing: { after: 80 },
      children: [new TextRun({ text: `OCR Result: ${sourceName}`, bold: true })]
    });

    const subtitleParagraph = new Paragraph({
      spacing: { after: 240 },
      children: [
        new TextRun({
          text: `Extracted ${new Date().toLocaleDateString()} by DocuForge`,
          italics: true,
          color: '64748B',
          size: 18
        })
      ]
    });

    const paragraphs = text.split(/\r?\n/).map((line) => {
      const isHeader = line.startsWith('---');
      return new Paragraph({
        children: [
          new TextRun({
            text: line || ' ',
            bold: isHeader,
            color: isHeader ? '2563EB' : '1E293B',
            size: isHeader ? 24 : 22
          })
        ],
        spacing: { after: 120 }
      });
    });

    const doc = new Document({
      sections: [
        {
          properties: {},
          children:
            paragraphs.length > 0
              ? [titleParagraph, subtitleParagraph, ...paragraphs]
              : [titleParagraph, subtitleParagraph, new Paragraph({ text: 'No text extracted.' })]
        }
      ]
    });

    return await Packer.toBlob(doc);
  }
}

export { PDFPasswordError };

FILE: src/services/pdfEngine.ts
